using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
using SixLabors.ImageSharp;
using SixLabors.ImageSharp.PixelFormats;
using ZXing;
using ZXing.Common;

namespace X_ChemicalStorage.IRepository.ServicesRepository
{
    public class ServicesLot : IServicesRepository<Lot> , IServicesLot
    {
        private readonly UserManager<ApplicationUser> _userManager;
        private readonly IHttpContextAccessor _httpContextAccessor;
        private readonly ApplicationDbContext _context;
        private readonly IWebHostEnvironment _env;

        public ServicesLot(UserManager<ApplicationUser> userManager, IHttpContextAccessor httpContextAccessor, ApplicationDbContext context, IWebHostEnvironment env)
        {
            _userManager = userManager;
            _httpContextAccessor = httpContextAccessor;
            _context = context;
            _env = env;
        }
        private void DeleteLotHard(Lot lot)
        {
            // 1️⃣ Delete barcode file
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(lot.BarcodeImage))
            {
                var barcodePath = Path.Combine(
                    _env.WebRootPath,
                    lot.BarcodeImage.TrimStart('/')
                );

                if (File.Exists(barcodePath))
                {
                    File.Delete(barcodePath);
                }
            }

            // 2️⃣ Hard delete from DB
            _context.Lots.Remove(lot);
        }

        #region List Lots
        public List<Lot> GetAll()
        {
            try
            {
                return _context.Lots
                    .Include(x=>x.Item)
                    .Include(x=>x.Suppliers)
                    .Include(x => x.Box)
                    .ThenInclude(b => b.Shelf)
                        .ThenInclude(s => s.StorageUnit)
                            .ThenInclude(u => u.Room)
                    .Where(x => x.CurrentState > 0 && x.AvilableQuantity > 0)
                    .OrderBy(x=>x.ExpiryDate)
                    .ToList();
            }
            catch
            {
                return new List<Lot>();
            }
        }
        #endregion

        #region Find Lot by ...
        //FindCenterBy => Id | Name 
        public Lot FindBy(int Id) => _context.Lots.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id.Equals(Id) && x.CurrentState > 0);
        public Lot FindBy(string Number) => _context.Lots.FirstOrDefault(x => x.LotNumber.Equals(Number) && x.CurrentState > 0);

        #endregion

        #region Create Lot (Add & Update)
        // Add | Update Lot
        public bool Save(Lot model)
        {
            try
            {
                var result = FindBy(model.Id);
                if (result == null)
                {
                    // Add record to Lot 

                   // model.LotNumber = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N").Substring(0, 8);
                    //Generate Barcode !
                    var writer = new BarcodeWriterPixelData
                    {
                        Format = BarcodeFormat.CODE_128,
                        Options = new EncodingOptions
                        {
                            Width = 300,
                            Height = 120,
                            Margin = 2
                        }
                    };

                    var pixelData = writer.Write(model.LotNumber);

                    using var image = Image.LoadPixelData<Rgba32>(
                        pixelData.Pixels,
                        pixelData.Width,
                        pixelData.Height);

                    string folderPath = Path.Combine(_env.WebRootPath, "lotbarcodes");
                    if (!Directory.Exists(folderPath))
                        Directory.CreateDirectory(folderPath);

                    string fileName = model.LotNumber + ".png";
                    string filePath = Path.Combine(folderPath, fileName);
                    image.Save(filePath);

                    // Add Lot Record
                    model.ReceivedDate = DateTime.Now;
                    model.BarcodeImage = "/lotbarcodes/" + fileName;
                    model.AvilableQuantity = model.TotalQuantity;
                    model.CurrentState = (int)Helper.eCurrentState.Active;
                    _context.Lots.Add(model);
                    _context.SaveChanges();

                    //Update Avilable Quantity after Transaction in Item Table
                    Item item = _context.Items.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == model.ItemId);
                    if (item != null)
                    {
                        item.AvilableQuantity += model.AvilableQuantity;
                        _context.Items.Update(item);
                    }
                    _context.SaveChanges();


                    // Add record to Lot Transaction
                    var trans = new LotTransaction();
                    int maxTr = _context.LotTransactions.Count() + 1;
                    trans.Move_Num = maxTr;
                    trans.Move_Statement = "Add New Lot :" + model.LotNumber;
                    trans.Move_Quantity = model.AvilableQuantity;
                    trans.Move_Date = DateTime.Now.Date;
                    trans.LotId = model.Id;
                    trans.Move_State = true;
                    trans.Total_Quantity = model.AvilableQuantity;

                    trans.CreatedBy = _userManager.GetUserAsync(_httpContextAccessor.HttpContext?.User).Result.FullName;
                    trans.DeviceUsing = Environment.MachineName;

                    trans.CurrentState = (int)Helper.eCurrentState.Active;
                    _context.LotTransactions.Add(trans);
                    _context.SaveChanges();

                    // Add Item Transaction Record 
                    var itemTrans = new ItemTransaction();
                    
                    int maxTrItem = _context.ItemTransactions.Count() + 1;
                    itemTrans.Move_Num = maxTrItem;
                    itemTrans.Move_Statement = "Add Lot Num : "+model.LotNumber;
                    itemTrans.Move_Quantity = model.AvilableQuantity;
                    itemTrans.Move_Date = DateTime.Now.Date;
                    itemTrans.Item = item;
                    itemTrans.ItemId = item.Id;
                    itemTrans.Move_State = true;
                    itemTrans.Total_Quantity = item.AvilableQuantity;

                    itemTrans.CreatedBy = _userManager.GetUserAsync(_httpContextAccessor.HttpContext?.User).Result.FullName;
                    itemTrans.DeviceUsing = Environment.MachineName;

                    itemTrans.CurrentState = (int)Helper.eCurrentState.Active;
                    _context.ItemTransactions.Add(itemTrans);
                    _context.SaveChanges();
                }

                ///////////// Update Lot ////////////////
                else
                {
                    result.LotNumber = model.LotNumber;
                    result.TotalQuantity = model.TotalQuantity;
                    result.AvilableQuantity = model.AvilableQuantity;
                    result.ReceivedDate = model.ReceivedDate;
                    result.ManufactureDate = model.ManufactureDate;
                    result.ExpiryDate = model.ExpiryDate;
                    result.SDS = model.SDS;
                    result.BoxId = model.BoxId;
                    result.RowIndex = model.RowIndex;
                    result.ColumnIndex = model.ColumnIndex;

                    result.CurrentState = (int)Helper.eCurrentState.Active;
                    _context.Lots.Update(result);
                }
                _context.SaveChanges();
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }
        #endregion

        #region Exchange Lot 
        // Exchange Lot
        public bool Exchange(Lot model)
        {
            using var dbTransaction = _context.Database.BeginTransaction();

            try
            {
                var lot = _context.Lots
                    .Include(l => l.Item)
                    .FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id == model.Id);

                if (lot == null)
                    return false;

                if (model.ExchageQuantity <= 0 ||
                    model.ExchageQuantity > lot.AvilableQuantity)
                    return false;

                // ================= 1️⃣ Update Quantities =================
                lot.AvilableQuantity -= model.ExchageQuantity;
                lot.Item.AvilableQuantity -= model.ExchageQuantity;

                _context.Lots.Update(lot);
                _context.Items.Update(lot.Item);

                // ================= 2️⃣ Lot Transaction =================
                var lotTrans = new LotTransaction
                {
                    Move_Num = _context.LotTransactions.Count() + 1,
                    Move_Statement = "Exchange Lot : " + lot.LotNumber,
                    Move_Quantity = model.ExchageQuantity,
                    Move_Date = DateTime.Now,
                    Move_State = false,
                    Total_Quantity = lot.AvilableQuantity,
                    LotId = lot.Id,
                    Recipient = model.Recipient,
                    CreatedBy = _userManager.GetUserAsync(
                        _httpContextAccessor.HttpContext.User).Result.FullName,
                    DeviceUsing = Environment.MachineName,
                    CurrentState = (int)Helper.eCurrentState.Active
                };

                _context.LotTransactions.Add(lotTrans);

                // ================= 3️⃣ Item Transaction =================
                var itemTrans = new ItemTransaction
                {
                    Move_Num = _context.ItemTransactions.Count() + 1,
                    Move_Statement = "Exchange Lot : " + lot.LotNumber,
                    Move_Quantity = model.ExchageQuantity,
                    Move_Date = DateTime.Now,
                    Move_State = false,
                    Total_Quantity = lot.Item.AvilableQuantity,
                    ItemId = lot.ItemId,
                    CreatedBy = lotTrans.CreatedBy,
                    DeviceUsing = lotTrans.DeviceUsing,
                    CurrentState = (int)Helper.eCurrentState.Active
                };

                _context.ItemTransactions.Add(itemTrans);

                // ================= 4️⃣ Auto Delete Lot =================
                if (lot.AvilableQuantity == 0)
                {
                    var deleteItemTrans = new ItemTransaction
                    {
                        Move_Num = _context.ItemTransactions.Count() + 2,
                        Move_Statement = "Auto Delete Lot (Quantity = 0)",
                        Move_Quantity = 0,
                        Move_Date = DateTime.Now,
                        Move_State = false,
                        Total_Quantity = lot.Item.AvilableQuantity,
                        ItemId = lot.ItemId,
                        CreatedBy = lotTrans.CreatedBy,
                        DeviceUsing = lotTrans.DeviceUsing,
                        CurrentState = (int)Helper.eCurrentState.Active
                    };

                    _context.ItemTransactions.Add(deleteItemTrans);

                    var deleteTrans = new LotTransaction
                    {
                        Move_Num = _context.LotTransactions.Count() + 2,
                        Move_Statement = "Auto Delete Lot (Quantity = 0)",
                        Move_Quantity = 0,
                        Move_Date = DateTime.Now,
                        Move_State = false,
                        Total_Quantity = 0,
                        LotId = lot.Id,
                        CreatedBy = lotTrans.CreatedBy,
                        DeviceUsing = lotTrans.DeviceUsing,
                        CurrentState = (int)Helper.eCurrentState.Active
                    };

                    _context.LotTransactions.Add(deleteTrans);

                    // Delete barcode image
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(lot.BarcodeImage))
                    {
                        var barcodePath = Path.Combine(
                            _env.WebRootPath,
                            lot.BarcodeImage.TrimStart('/'));

                        if (File.Exists(barcodePath))
                            File.Delete(barcodePath);
                    }

                    _context.Lots.Remove(lot);
                }

                _context.SaveChanges();
                dbTransaction.Commit();

                return true;
            }
            catch
            {
                dbTransaction.Rollback();
                return false;
            }
        }

        #endregion

        #region Delete Lot
        public bool Delete(int Id)
        {
            try
            {
                var result = FindBy(Id);
                if (result != null)
                {
                    result.CurrentState = (int)Helper.eCurrentState.Delete;
                    _context.Lots.Update(result);
                    _context.SaveChanges();
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }
        #endregion

        #region Lot Details
        // Lot Details
        public Lot Details(int Id)
        {
            try
            {
                return _context.Lots.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id.Equals(Id) && x.CurrentState > 0);
            }
            catch
            {
                return null;
            }
        }
        #endregion

        

        #region List Transactions Of Lot
        public List<LotTransaction> GetLotTransactionsOfLot(int id)
        {
            try
            {
                return _context.LotTransactions
                                     .Where(x => x.CurrentState > 0 && x.LotId == id)
                                     .ToList();
            }
            catch
            {
                return new List<LotTransaction>();
            }
        }
        #endregion
    }
}



namespace X_ChemicalStorage.IRepository.ServicesRepository
{
    public class ServicesManufacuterCompany : IServicesRepository<ManufacuterCompany>
    {
        private readonly ApplicationDbContext _context;

        public ServicesManufacuterCompany(ApplicationDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }

        #region List ManufacuterCompanies
        public List<ManufacuterCompany> GetAll()
        {
            try
            {
                return _context.ManufacuterCompanies.Where(x => x.CurrentState > 0).ToList();
            }
            catch
            {
                return new List<ManufacuterCompany>();
            }
        }
        #endregion

        #region Find ManufacuterCompany by ...
        //FindCenterBy => Id | Name 
        public ManufacuterCompany FindBy(int Id) => _context.ManufacuterCompanies.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id.Equals(Id) && x.CurrentState > 0);
        public ManufacuterCompany FindBy(string Name) => _context.ManufacuterCompanies.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Name.Equals(Name.Trim()) && x.CurrentState > 0);

        #endregion

        #region Save ManufacuterCompany (Add & Update)
        // Add | Update ManufacuterCompany
        public bool Save(ManufacuterCompany model)
        {
            try
            {
                var result = FindBy(model.Id);
                if (result == null)
                {
                    model.CurrentState = (int)Helper.eCurrentState.Active;
                    _context.ManufacuterCompanies.Add(model);
                }
                else
                {
                    result.Name = model.Name;
                    result.ShortCutName = model.ShortCutName;
                    result.Details = model.Details;
                    result.CurrentState = (int)Helper.eCurrentState.Active;
                    _context.ManufacuterCompanies.Update(result);
                }
                _context.SaveChanges();
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }
        #endregion

        #region Delete ManufacuterCompany
        public bool Delete(int Id)
        {
            try
            {
                var result = FindBy(Id);
                if (result != null)
                {
                    result.CurrentState = (int)Helper.eCurrentState.Delete;
                    _context.ManufacuterCompanies.Update(result);
                    _context.SaveChanges();
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {
                return false;
            }
        }
        #endregion

        #region ManufacuterCompany Details
        // ManufacuterCompany Details
        public ManufacuterCompany Details(int Id)
        {
            try
            {
                return _context.ManufacuterCompanies.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Id.Equals(Id) && x.CurrentState > 0);
            }
            catch
            {
                return null;
            }
        }
        #endregion
    }
}

using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations.Schema;
namespace X_ChemicalStorage.Models
{
    public class Lot
    {
        [DatabaseGenerated(DatabaseGeneratedOption.Identity)]
        public int Id { get; set; }
        [NotMapped]
        public string ShortLocation =>
            Box != null
                ? $"{Box.Shelf.StorageUnit.Room.Name} / {Box.Shelf.StorageUnit.Name} / {Box.Name} (R{RowIndex},C{ColumnIndex})"
                : "Not Assigned";
        public string? LotNumber { get; set; }
        public double? ExchageQuantity { get; set; }
        public string? Recipient { get; set; } // جهة الاستلام
        public double? TotalQuantity { get; set; }
        public double? AvilableQuantity { get; set; }
        [DataType(DataType.Date)]
        [DisplayFormat(DataFormatString = "{0:yyyy-MM-dd}", ApplyFormatInEditMode = true)]
        public DateTime? ReceivedDate { get; set; }
        
        [DataType(DataType.Date)]
        [DisplayFormat(DataFormatString = "{0:yyyy-MM-dd}", ApplyFormatInEditMode = true)]
        public DateTime? ManufactureDate { get; set; }
        
        [DataType(DataType.Date)]
        [DisplayFormat(DataFormatString = "{0:yyyy-MM-dd}", ApplyFormatInEditMode = true)]
        public DateTime? ExpiryDate { get; set; }
        public bool? SDS { get; set; }
        public string? BarcodeImage { get; set; } // مسار صورة الباركود

        public int BoxId { get; set; }
        public Box Box { get; set; } = null!;

        public int RowIndex { get; set; }
        public int ColumnIndex { get; set; }

        public int? ItemId { get; set; } 
        [ForeignKey("ItemId")]
        public Item? Item { get; set; }
        public ICollection<Supplier> Suppliers { get; set; } = new List<Supplier>();
        //public ICollection<SupplierLot> SupplierLots { get; set; }
        public int? CurrentState { get; set; }
    }
}

using Analyzer.Utilities;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations.Schema;
using static X_ChemicalStorage.Constants.Permissions;

namespace X_ChemicalStorage.Models
{
    public class Substance
    {
        [DatabaseGenerated(DatabaseGeneratedOption.Identity)]
        public int Id { get; set; }
        public string? Substance_Name { get; set; } // Substance Name
        public string? IUPAC_Name { get; set; } // IUPAC Name
        public string? CAS_Number { get; set; } // Chemical Abstracts Service number
        public string? Synonym { get; set; }  

        public string? Chemical_Formula { get; set; } // Chemical Formula
        public string? Physical_State { get; set; } // Physical State (e.g., solid, liquid, gas)
        public string? GHS_Classification { get; set; } // GHS Classification
        public string? Hazard_Statements { get; set; } // Hazard Statements
        public double? Size { get; set; } // Hazard Statements
        public double? Concentration { get; set; } // concentration
        public string? VendorName { get; set; } // Vendor Name

        // Foreign Keys
        public int? CategoryId { get; set; }
        [ForeignKey("CategoryId")]
        public Category? Category { get; set; }
        // Foreign Keys
        public int? UnitId { get; set; }
        [ForeignKey("UnitId")]
        public Unit? Unit { get; set; }
        // Foreign Keys
        public int? LocationId { get; set; } // Location Details
        [ForeignKey("LocationId")]
        public Location? Location { get; set; }
        // Foreign Keys
        public int? SupplierId { get; set; } // Supplier Name
        [ForeignKey("SupplierId")]
        public Supplier? Supplier { get; set; }
        // Foreign Keys
        public int? ManufacturerId { get; set; } // Manufacturer Id
        [ForeignKey("ManufacturerId")]
        public ManufacuterCompany? Manufacturer { get; set; }

        public ICollection<Lot> Lots { get; set; } = new List<Lot>();

        public int? CurrentState { get; set; }
    }
}

==================== Seeds =========================



namespace X_ChemicalStorage.Seeds
{
    public static class DefaultRoles
    {
        public static async Task SeedAsync(UserManager<ApplicationUser> userManager, RoleManager<ApplicationRole> roleManager)
        {
            if (!roleManager.Roles.Any())
            {
                await roleManager.CreateAsync(new ApplicationRole(Roles.Admin.ToString()));
                await roleManager.CreateAsync(new ApplicationRole(Roles.User.ToString()));
            }
        }
    }
}




namespace X_ChemicalStorage.Seeds
{
    public static class DefaultUsers
    {
        //Default Parent
        public static async Task SeedUserAsync(UserManager<ApplicationUser> userManager, RoleManager<ApplicationRole> roleManager)
        {
            //Seed Default User
            var defaultUser = new ApplicationUser
            {

                UserName = "user",
                Email = "user@test.com",
                NormalizedUserName = "user".ToUpper(),
                NormalizedEmail = "user@test.com".ToUpper(),
                EmailConfirmed = true,
                PhoneNumber = "01000000000",
                FullName = "User",
            };
            if (userManager.Users.All(u => u.Id != defaultUser.Id))
            {
                var user = await userManager.FindByEmailAsync(defaultUser.Email);
                if (user == null)
                {
                    await userManager.CreateAsync(defaultUser, "$Aa123123");
                    await userManager.AddToRoleAsync(defaultUser, Roles.User.ToString());
                }
                //await roleManager.SeedClaimsForUser();
            }
        }

        //Default Admin
        public static async Task SeedAdminAsync(UserManager<ApplicationUser> userManager, RoleManager<ApplicationRole> roleManager)
        {
            //Seed Default User
            var defaultUser = new ApplicationUser
            {
                UserName = "admin",
                Email = "admin@test.com",
                NormalizedUserName = "admin".ToUpper(),
                NormalizedEmail = "admin@test.com".ToUpper(),
                EmailConfirmed = true,
                PhoneNumber = "02000000000",
                FullName = "Admin",
            };
            if (userManager.Users.All(u => u.Id != defaultUser.Id))
            {
                var user = await userManager.FindByEmailAsync(defaultUser.Email);
                if (user == null)
                {
                    await userManager.CreateAsync(defaultUser, "$Aa123123");
                    await userManager.AddToRoleAsync(defaultUser, Roles.Admin.ToString());
                }
                //await roleManager.SeedClaimsForAdmin();
            }
        }


        private async static Task SeedClaimsForAdmin(this RoleManager<IdentityRole> roleManager)
        {
            var adminRole = await roleManager.FindByNameAsync("Admin");
            await roleManager.AddPermissionClaim(adminRole, "");
        }
        private async static Task SeedClaimsForUser(this RoleManager<IdentityRole> roleManager)
        {
            var userRole = await roleManager.FindByNameAsync("User");
            await roleManager.AddPermissionClaim(userRole, "");
        }

        public static async Task AddPermissionClaim(this RoleManager<IdentityRole> roleManager, IdentityRole role, string module)
        {
            var allClaims = await roleManager.GetClaimsAsync(role);
            var allPermissions = Permissions.GeneratePermissionsList(module);
            foreach (var permission in allPermissions)
            {
                if (!allClaims.Any(a => a.Type == "Permission" && a.Value == permission))
                {
                    await roleManager.AddClaimAsync(role, new Claim("Permission", permission));
                }
            }
        }
    }
}



================================= Services =============================

using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace X_ChemicalStorage.Services
{
    public class DashboardService : IDashboardService
    {
        private readonly ApplicationDbContext _context;
        public DashboardService(ApplicationDbContext context)
        {
            _context = context;
        }
        public DashboardViewModel GetDashboard()
        {
            var totalItems = _context.Items.Count();
            var totalLots = _context.Lots.Count();

            var totalAvailableQty = _context.Items
                .Sum(i => (float?)i.AvilableQuantity) ?? 0;

            // 🔴 Under Limit
            var underLimitItems = _context.Items
                .Count(i => i.AvilableQuantity <= i.Limit);

            // ⏳ Expiry 
            var expiringLots = _context.Lots
                .Count(l => l.ExpiryDate <= DateTime.Today.AddDays(0)
                         && l.AvilableQuantity > 0);

            // ⏳ Near Expiry (30 days)
            var nearexpiringLots = _context.Lots
                .Count(l => l.ExpiryDate <= DateTime.Today.AddDays(30)
                         && l.AvilableQuantity > 0);

            // ☣️ Hazard (SDS)
            var hazardItems = _context.Items
                .Count(i => i.SDS==false);


            // Chart (Top Items)
            var chartData = _context.Items
                .OrderByDescending(i => i.AvilableQuantity)
                .Take(10)
                .Select(i => new
                {
                    i.Name,
                    Qty = i.AvilableQuantity
                })
                .ToList();

            return new DashboardViewModel
            {
                TotalItems = totalItems,
                TotalLots = totalLots,
                TotalAvailableQty = totalAvailableQty,
                UnderLimitItems = underLimitItems,
                ExpiringLots = expiringLots,
                NearExpiringLots = nearexpiringLots,
                HazardItems = hazardItems,

                ItemNames = chartData.Select(x => x.Name).ToList(),
                ItemQuantities = chartData.Select(x => x.Qty).ToList()
            };
        }
    }
}

using X_ChemicalStorage.Dtos;

namespace X_ChemicalStorage.ViewModels
{
    public class DashboardViewModel
    {
        //// KPIs
        public int TotalItems { get; set; }
        public int TotalLots { get; set; }

        public double? TotalAvailableQty { get; set; }
        public int UnderLimitItems { get; set; }
        public int ExpiringLots { get; set; }
        public int NearExpiringLots { get; set; }
        public int HazardItems { get; set; }

        // Alerts Items
        public bool HasUnderLimitAlert => UnderLimitItems > 0;
        public bool HasExpiryAlert => ExpiringLots > 0;
        public bool HasNearExpiryAlert => NearExpiringLots > 0;
        public bool HasHazardAlert => HazardItems > 0;

        // Charts
        public List<string> ItemNames { get; set; } = new();
        public List<double?> ItemQuantities { get; set; } = new();
        public List<CategorySummaryVm> CategorySummary { get; set; } = new();
        public List<SdsWaveItemVm> SdsWaveItems { get; set; }
        public StorageConditionSummaryVm StorageCondition { get; set; }

        public List<TimeSeriesPointVm> LotsOverTime { get; set; }
        public List<TimeSeriesPointVm> ExpiryTrend { get; set; }

    }
    public class SdsWaveItemVm
    {
        public string ItemName { get; set; }
        public int Quantity { get; set; }
        public bool IsValid { get; set; }
    }
    public class StorageConditionSummaryVm
    {
        public int RoomTempCount { get; set; }
        public int FreezerCount { get; set; }
        public int ColdCount { get; set; }

        public int Total =>
            RoomTempCount + FreezerCount + ColdCount;

        public int RoomTempPercent =>
            Total == 0 ? 0 : (RoomTempCount * 100 / Total);

        public int FreezerPercent =>
            Total == 0 ? 0 : (FreezerCount * 100 / Total);

        public int ColdPercent =>
            Total == 0 ? 0 : (ColdCount * 100 / Total);
    }
    public class CategorySummaryVm
    {
        public string CategoryName { get; set; }
        public int ItemsCount { get; set; }
    }
    public class TimeSeriesPointVm
    {
        public string Label { get; set; }   // Month / Date
        public int Value { get; set; }
    }

}

        ////// Permissions ////////////
        ///

        public class LotDisplayViewModel
        {

            

            public Guid Id { get; set; }

            public string ItemName { get; set; }
            public string ItemCode { get; set; }

            public string LotNumber { get; set; }
            public string BarcodeImage { get; set; }

            public DateTime ExpiryDate { get; set; }
            public DateTime ManufactureDate { get; set; }
            public DateTime ReceivedDate { get; set; }

            public int AvailableQuantity { get; set; }
            public int RoomNumber { get; set; }

            public bool SDS { get; set; }

            /* ================== Calculated ================== */

            public int DaysLeft =>
                (ExpiryDate.Date - DateTime.Now.Date).Days;

            public bool IsExpired => DaysLeft <= 0;

            public bool IsNearExpiry =>
                DaysLeft > 0 && DaysLeft <= 30;

            // نفس ألوان الديسكتوب
            public string ExpiryTextClass =>
                IsExpired ? "link-danger fw-bold" :
                IsNearExpiry ? "link-warning fw-bold" :
                "link-success fw-bold";

            public string ExpiryBorderColor =>
                IsExpired ? "#f94e3b" :
                IsNearExpiry ? "#ffc107" :
                "#4caf50";

            public string SDSBadgeClass =>
                SDS ? "bg-success-subtle link-success" :
                      "bg-danger-subtle link-danger";

            public string SDSStatusText =>
                SDS ? "Valid" : "Invalid";
        }
    }

@section Scripts {
    <script src="~/js/biobank.js"></script>
    <script>
        var demoData = {
            freezers: [
                { id: "FZ80-01", type: "-80C", temp: -80, shelves: 4, racksPerShelf: 4, drawersPerRack: 5, boxesPerDrawer: 5, tubesPerBox: 100, occupancy: 72, status: "ONLINE", barcode: "FZ80-01-BC" },
                { id: "FZ80-02", type: "-80C", temp: -80, shelves: 4, racksPerShelf: 4, drawersPerRack: 5, boxesPerDrawer: 5, tubesPerBox: 100, occupancy: 65, status: "ONLINE", barcode: "FZ80-02-BC" },
                { id: "FZ80-03", type: "-80C", temp: -80, shelves: 4, racksPerShelf: 4, drawersPerRack: 5, boxesPerDrawer: 5, tubesPerBox: 100, occupancy: 88, status: "ONLINE", barcode: "FZ80-03-BC" },
                { id: "FZ80-04", type: "-80C", temp: -80, shelves: 4, racksPerShelf: 4, drawersPerRack: 5, boxesPerDrawer: 5, tubesPerBox: 100, occupancy: 45, status: "ONLINE", barcode: "FZ80-04-BC" },
                { id: "FZ150-01", type: "-150C", temp: -150, shelves: 0, racksPerShelf: 12, drawersPerRack: 0, boxesPerDrawer: 1, tubesPerBox: 100, occupancy: 92, status: "ONLINE", barcode: "FZ150-01-BC" },
                { id: "FZ150-02", type: "-150C", temp: -150, shelves: 0, racksPerShelf: 12, drawersPerRack: 0, boxesPerDrawer: 1, tubesPerBox: 100, occupancy: 78, status: "ONLINE", barcode: "FZ150-02-BC" }
            ]
        };

        var currentView = "room";
        var currentPath = [];

        function generateId(prefix, parentId, index) {
            return parentId + "-" + prefix + String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0");
        }
        function formatNumber(n) { return n.toLocaleString(); }
        function getOccupancyColor(pct) {
            if (pct < 50) return "var(--bb-success)";
            if (pct < 80) return "var(--bb-warning)";
            return "var(--bb-danger)";
        }
        function findFreezer(id) {
            return demoData.freezers.find(function (f) { return f.id === id; });
        }

        function navigateTo(view, data) {
            currentView = view;
            var viewport = document.getElementById("bbViewport");
            viewport.innerHTML = "";
            viewport.className = "bb-viewport bb-fade-in";

            switch (view) {
                case "room": renderRoom(); currentPath = []; break;
                case "freezer": renderFreezer(data); currentPath = [data]; break;
                case "shelf": renderShelf(data.freezer, data.shelfIndex); currentPath = [data.freezer, data]; break;
                case "rack": renderRack(data.freezer, data.shelfIndex, data.rackIndex); currentPath = [data.freezer, { shelfIndex: data.shelfIndex }, data]; break;
                case "drawer": renderDrawer(data.freezer, data.shelfIndex, data.rackIndex, data.drawerIndex); currentPath = [data.freezer, { shelfIndex: data.shelfIndex }, { rackIndex: data.rackIndex }, data]; break;
                case "box": renderBox(data); currentPath = [data.freezer, { shelfIndex: data.shelfIndex }, { rackIndex: data.rackIndex }, { drawerIndex: data.drawerIndex }, data]; break;
            }

            updateBreadcrumb();
            updateMinimap();
            hideInfoPanel();
        }

        function updateBreadcrumb() {
            var bc = document.getElementById("bbBreadcrumb");
            var html = `<div class="bb-bcrumb-item ${currentView === "room" ? "active" : ""}" data-nav="room"><span>&#127970;</span> Bio Bank Room</div>`;

            if (currentView !== "room" && currentPath[0]) {
                var fz = currentPath[0];
                html += `<span class="bb-bcrumb-sep">&#8250;</span>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-bcrumb-item ${currentView === "freezer" ? "active" : ""}" data-nav="freezer" data-freezer-id="${fz.id}"><span>&#10052;</span> ${fz.id}</div>`;
            }

            if ((currentView === "shelf" || currentView === "rack" || currentView === "drawer" || currentView === "box") && currentPath[1]) {
                var sIdx = currentPath[1].shelfIndex || 0;
                html += `<span class="bb-bcrumb-sep">&#8250;</span>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-bcrumb-item ${currentView === "shelf" ? "active" : ""}" data-nav="shelf" data-shelf-index="${sIdx}"><span>&#128218;</span> S${String(sIdx + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</div>`;
            }

            if ((currentView === "rack" || currentView === "drawer" || currentView === "box") && currentPath[2]) {
                var rIdx = currentPath[2].rackIndex || 0;
                html += `<span class="bb-bcrumb-sep">&#8250;</span>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-bcrumb-item ${currentView === "rack" ? "active" : ""}" data-nav="rack" data-shelf-index="${currentPath[1].shelfIndex}" data-rack-index="${rIdx}"><span>&#128295;</span> R${String(rIdx + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</div>`;
            }

            if ((currentView === "drawer" || currentView === "box") && currentPath[3]) {
                var dIdx = currentPath[3].drawerIndex || 0;
                html += `<span class="bb-bcrumb-sep">&#8250;</span>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-bcrumb-item ${currentView === "drawer" ? "active" : ""}" data-nav="drawer" data-shelf-index="${currentPath[1].shelfIndex}" data-rack-index="${currentPath[2].rackIndex}" data-drawer-index="${dIdx}"><span>&#128448;</span> D${String(dIdx + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</div>`;
            }

            if (currentView === "box" && currentPath[4]) {
                var boxId = currentPath[4].boxId || "BX01";
                html += `<span class="bb-bcrumb-sep">&#8250;</span>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-bcrumb-item active"><span>&#128230;</span> ${boxId}</div>`;
            }

            bc.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function updateMinimap() {
            var mm = document.getElementById("minimapContent");
            var html = "";

            demoData.freezers.forEach(function (fz) {
                var isActive = currentPath[0] && currentPath[0].id === fz.id;
                html += `<div class="bb-tree-item ${isActive ? "active" : ""}" data-nav="freezer" data-freezer-id="${fz.id}">`;
                html += `<span class="bb-tree-icon">&#10052;</span> ${fz.id}</div>`;

                if (isActive && fz.type === "-80C") {
                    html += `<div class="bb-tree-children">`;
                    for (var s = 0; s < fz.shelves; s++) {
                        var sId = "S" + String(s + 1).padStart(2, "0");
                        var sActive = currentPath[1] && currentPath[1].shelfIndex === s;
                        html += `<div class="bb-tree-item ${sActive ? "active" : ""}" data-nav="shelf" data-shelf-index="${s}">`;
                        html += `<span class="bb-tree-icon">&#128218;</span> ${sId}</div>`;

                        if (sActive) {
                            html += `<div class="bb-tree-children">`;
                            for (var r = 0; r < fz.racksPerShelf; r++) {
                                var rId = "R" + String(r + 1).padStart(2, "0");
                                var rActive = currentPath[2] && currentPath[2].rackIndex === r;
                                html += `<div class="bb-tree-item ${rActive ? "active" : ""}" data-nav="rack" data-shelf-index="${s}" data-rack-index="${r}">`;
                                html += `<span class="bb-tree-icon">&#128295;</span> ${rId}</div>`;
                            }
                            html += `</div>`;
                        }
                    }
                    html += `</div>`;
                }

                if (isActive && fz.type === "-150C") {
                    html += `<div class="bb-tree-children">`;
                    for (var r2 = 0; r2 < fz.racksPerShelf; r2++) {
                        var rId2 = "R" + String(r2 + 1).padStart(2, "0");
                        html += `<div class="bb-tree-item" data-nav="rack" data-shelf-index="0" data-rack-index="${r2}">`;
                        html += `<span class="bb-tree-icon">&#128295;</span> ${rId2}</div>`;
                    }
                    html += `</div>`;
                }
            });

            mm.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function renderRoom() {
            var container = document.getElementById("bbViewport");
            var html = `<div class="bb-room">`;

            demoData.freezers.forEach(function (fz) {
                var totalCap = fz.type === "-80C"
                    ? fz.shelves * fz.racksPerShelf * fz.drawersPerRack * fz.boxesPerDrawer * fz.tubesPerBox
                    : fz.racksPerShelf * fz.boxesPerDrawer * fz.tubesPerBox;

                var occColor = getOccupancyColor(fz.occupancy);
                var shelfBars = "";
                if (fz.type === "-80C") {
                    for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) shelfBars += `<div class="bb-fz-shelf"></div>`;
                } else {
                    shelfBars = `<div class="bb-fz-shelf" style="flex:0.3"></div>`;
                }

                html += `<div class="bb-freezer" data-nav="freezer" data-hover="freezer" data-freezer-id="${fz.id}">`;
                html += `<div class="bb-freezer-header">`;
                html += `<div class="bb-freezer-id">${fz.id}</div>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-temp-badge ${fz.type === "-150C" ? "cold" : ""}">&#127777; ${fz.temp}C</div>`;
                html += `</div>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-freezer-body">`;
                html += `<div class="bb-freezer-visual">`;
                html += `<div class="bb-freezer-door"></div>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-freezer-handle"></div>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-freezer-shelves">${shelfBars}</div>`;
                html += `</div>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-freezer-stats">`;
                html += `<div class="bb-stat-row"><span class="bb-stat-label">Type</span><span class="bb-stat-value">${fz.type}</span></div>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-stat-row"><span class="bb-stat-label">Shelves</span><span class="bb-stat-value">${fz.shelves || "-"}</span></div>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-stat-row"><span class="bb-stat-label">Racks</span><span class="bb-stat-value">${fz.type === "-80C" ? fz.shelves * fz.racksPerShelf : fz.racksPerShelf}</span></div>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-stat-row"><span class="bb-stat-label">Capacity</span><span class="bb-stat-value">${formatNumber(totalCap)} tubes</span></div>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-occupancy-bar">`;
                html += `<div class="bb-occ-label"><span>Occupancy</span><span style="color:${occColor}">${fz.occupancy}%</span></div>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-occ-track"><div class="bb-occ-fill" style="width:${fz.occupancy}%; background: linear-gradient(90deg, ${occColor}, var(--bb-accent))"></div></div>`;
                html += `</div>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-freezer-status"><span class="bb-status-dot"></span> ${fz.status}</div>`;
                html += `</div></div></div>`;
            });

            html += `</div>`;
            container.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function renderFreezer(fz) {
            var container = document.getElementById("bbViewport");

            if (fz.type === "-150C") {
                var html = `<div class="bb-racks-container bb-zoom-in">`;
                for (var r = 0; r < fz.racksPerShelf; r++) {
                    var rackId = generateId("R", fz.id, r);
                    var occ = Math.floor(Math.random() * 40) + 60;
                    html += `<div class="bb-rack" data-nav="rack" data-hover="rack" data-shelf-index="0" data-rack-index="${r}" data-rack-id="${rackId}" data-occ="${occ}">`;
                    html += `<div class="bb-rack-visual"><div class="bb-rack-drawer" style="top:8px"></div></div>`;
                    html += `<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:4px;">${rackId}</div>`;
                    html += `<div style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--bb-text-muted);">1 Box - 100 Tubes</div>`;
                    html += `<div style="margin-top:8px;"><div class="bb-occ-track"><div class="bb-occ-fill" style="width:${occ}%"></div></div></div>`;
                    html += `</div>`;
                }
                html += `</div>`;
                container.innerHTML = html;
                return;
            }

            var html = `<div class="bb-shelves-container bb-zoom-in">`;
            for (var s = 0; s < fz.shelves; s++) {
                var shelfId = generateId("S", fz.id, s);
                var totalRacks = fz.racksPerShelf;
                var totalDrawers = totalRacks * fz.drawersPerRack;
                var totalBoxes = totalDrawers * fz.boxesPerDrawer;
                var totalTubes = totalBoxes * fz.tubesPerBox;
                var occ = Math.floor(Math.random() * 50) + 30;

                var rackSlots = "";
                for (var ri = 0; ri < totalRacks; ri++) rackSlots += `<div class="bb-shelf-rack-slot"></div>`;

                html += `<div class="bb-shelf" data-nav="shelf" data-hover="shelf" data-shelf-index="${s}" data-shelf-id="${shelfId}" data-racks="${totalRacks}" data-drawers="${totalDrawers}" data-boxes="${totalBoxes}" data-tubes="${totalTubes}" data-occ="${occ}">`;
                html += `<div class="bb-shelf-visual">${rackSlots}</div>`;
                html += `<div style="font-weight:700;font-size:1.1rem;margin-bottom:6px;">${shelfId}</div>`;
                html += `<div style="font-size:0.85rem;color:var(--bb-text-muted);margin-bottom:8px;">${totalRacks} Racks - ${totalDrawers} Drawers - ${totalBoxes} Boxes</div>`;
                html += `<div class="bb-occ-track"><div class="bb-occ-fill" style="width:${occ}%"></div></div>`;
                html += `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:0.8rem;margin-top:4px;">`;
                html += `<span style="color:var(--bb-text-muted)">Occupancy</span>`;
                html += `<span style="color:var(--bb-primary-light);font-weight:600">${occ}%</span></div>`;
                html += `</div>`;
            }
            html += `</div>`;
            container.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function renderShelf(fz, shelfIndex) {
            var container = document.getElementById("bbViewport");
            var html = `<div class="bb-racks-container bb-zoom-in">`;

            for (var r = 0; r < fz.racksPerShelf; r++) {
                var rackId = generateId("R", fz.id + "-S" + String(shelfIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0"), r);
                var occ = Math.floor(Math.random() * 50) + 30;

                var drawers = "";
                for (var d = 0; d < 5; d++) {
                    drawers += `<div class="bb-rack-drawer" style="top:${8 + d * 14}px"></div>`;
                }

                html += `<div class="bb-rack" data-nav="rack" data-hover="rack" data-shelf-index="${shelfIndex}" data-rack-index="${r}" data-rack-id="${rackId}" data-occ="${occ}">`;
                html += `<div class="bb-rack-visual">${drawers}</div>`;
                html += `<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:4px;">${rackId}</div>`;
                html += `<div style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--bb-text-muted);">${fz.drawersPerRack} Drawers - ${fz.drawersPerRack * fz.boxesPerDrawer} Boxes</div>`;
                html += `<div style="margin-top:8px;"><div class="bb-occ-track"><div class="bb-occ-fill" style="width:${occ}%"></div></div></div>`;
                html += `</div>`;
            }

            html += `</div>`;
            container.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function renderRack(fz, shelfIndex, rackIndex) {
            var container = document.getElementById("bbViewport");
            var html = `<div class="bb-drawers-container bb-zoom-in">`;

            var drawerCount = fz.type === "-150C" ? 1 : fz.drawersPerRack;

            for (var d = 0; d < drawerCount; d++) {
                var drawerId = generateId("D", fz.id + "-S" + String(shelfIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-R" + String(rackIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0"), d);
                var occ = Math.floor(Math.random() * 60) + 20;

                html += `<div class="bb-drawer" data-nav="drawer" data-hover="drawer" data-shelf-index="${shelfIndex}" data-rack-index="${rackIndex}" data-drawer-index="${d}" data-drawer-id="${drawerId}" data-occ="${occ}">`;
                html += `<div class="bb-drawer-visual"><div class="bb-drawer-handle"></div></div>`;
                html += `<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:4px;">${drawerId}</div>`;
                html += `<div style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--bb-text-muted);">${fz.boxesPerDrawer} Boxes - ${fz.boxesPerDrawer * fz.tubesPerBox} Tubes</div>`;
                html += `<div style="margin-top:8px;"><div class="bb-occ-track"><div class="bb-occ-fill" style="width:${occ}%"></div></div></div>`;
                html += `</div>`;
            }

            html += `</div>`;
            container.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function renderDrawer(fz, shelfIndex, rackIndex, drawerIndex) {
            var container = document.getElementById("bbViewport");
            var html = `<div class="bb-boxes-container bb-zoom-in">`;

            for (var b = 0; b < fz.boxesPerDrawer; b++) {
                var boxId = generateId("BX", fz.id + "-S" + String(shelfIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-R" + String(rackIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-D" + String(drawerIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0"), b);
                var occ = Math.floor(Math.random() * 100);
                var occCells = Math.floor(occ / 4);

                var cells = "";
                for (var c = 0; c < 25; c++) {
                    cells += `<div class="bb-box-cell ${c < occCells ? "occupied" : ""}"></div>`;
                }

                html += `<div class="bb-box" data-nav="box" data-hover="box" data-shelf-index="${shelfIndex}" data-rack-index="${rackIndex}" data-drawer-index="${drawerIndex}" data-box-id="${boxId}" data-box-index="${b}" data-occ="${occ}">`;
                html += `<div class="bb-box-visual">${cells}</div>`;
                html += `<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:4px;font-size:0.9rem;">${boxId}</div>`;
                html += `<div style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--bb-text-muted);">100 Tubes</div>`;
                html += `<div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:0.8rem;"><span style="color:${getOccupancyColor(occ)};font-weight:600">${occ}%</span> occupied</div>`;
                html += `</div>`;
            }

            html += `</div>`;
            container.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function renderBox(data) {
            var container = document.getElementById("bbViewport");
            var boxId = data.boxId;

            var colHeaders = "";
            for (var c = 0; c < 10; c++) {
                colHeaders += `<div style="text-align:center;font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--bb-text-muted);font-weight:600;">${c + 1}</div>`;
            }

            var rowLabels = "";
            for (var r = 0; r < 10; r++) {
                rowLabels += `<div style="height:32px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:0.7rem;color:var(--bb-text-muted);font-weight:600;">${String.fromCharCode(65 + r)}</div>`;
            }

            var tubes = "";
            for (var row = 0; row < 10; row++) {
                for (var col = 0; col < 10; col++) {
                    var pos = String.fromCharCode(65 + row) + String(col + 1).padStart(2, "0");
                    var isOccupied = Math.random() > 0.4;
                    var isReserved = !isOccupied && Math.random() > 0.8;
                    var tubeId = boxId + "-" + pos;
                    var stateClass = isOccupied ? "occupied" : (isReserved ? "reserved" : "");

                    tubes += `<div class="bb-tube ${stateClass}" data-hover="tube" data-action="open-modal" data-tube-id="${tubeId}" data-pos="${pos}" data-occupied="${isOccupied}" data-reserved="${isReserved}" title="${pos}"></div>`;
                }
            }

            var html = `<div class="bb-cryobox-container bb-zoom-in">`;
            html += `<div class="bb-cryobox">`;
            html += `<div class="bb-cryobox-header">`;
            html += `<div><div style="font-weight:700;font-size:1.2rem;">${boxId}</div>`;
            html += `<div style="font-size:0.8rem;color:var(--bb-text-muted);">Cryobox - 10x10 Grid - 100 Positions</div></div>`;
            html += `<div class="bb-temp-badge" style="font-size:0.75rem;">&#127777; ${data.freezer.temp}C</div>`;
            html += `</div>`;
            html += `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:30px 1fr;gap:8px;align-items:start;">`;
            html += `<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;padding-top:28px;">${rowLabels}</div>`;
            html += `<div>`;
            html += `<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(10,1fr);gap:6px;margin-bottom:6px;">${colHeaders}</div>`;
            html += `<div class="bb-cryobox-grid">${tubes}</div>`;
            html += `</div></div>`;
            html += `<div style="margin-top:16px;display:flex;gap:16px;justify-content:center;font-size:0.8rem;">`;
            html += `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;"><div style="width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:3px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--bb-accent),var(--bb-cyan));"></div><span style="color:var(--bb-text-muted)">Occupied</span></div>`;
            html += `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;"><div style="width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:3px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--bb-warning),var(--bb-gold));"></div><span style="color:var(--bb-text-muted)">Reserved</span></div>`;
            html += `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;"><div style="width:12px;height:12px;border-radius:3px;background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15);"></div><span style="color:var(--bb-text-muted)">Available</span></div>`;
            html += `</div></div></div>`;

            container.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function showFreezerInfo(fzId) {
            var fz = findFreezer(fzId);
            if (!fz) return;

            var totalCap = fz.type === "-80C"
                ? fz.shelves * fz.racksPerShelf * fz.drawersPerRack * fz.boxesPerDrawer * fz.tubesPerBox
                : fz.racksPerShelf * fz.boxesPerDrawer * fz.tubesPerBox;

            var occupied = Math.floor(totalCap * fz.occupancy / 100);

            document.getElementById("infoIcon").innerHTML = "&#10052;";
            document.getElementById("infoTitle").textContent = fz.id;
            document.getElementById("infoSubtitle").textContent = "Ultra-Low Temperature Freezer";
            document.getElementById("infoContent").innerHTML =
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Temperature</span><span class="value" style="color:var(--bb-cyan)">${fz.temp}C</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Type</span><span class="value">${fz.type}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Status</span><span class="value" style="color:var(--bb-success)">&#9679; ${fz.status}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Total Capacity</span><span class="value">${formatNumber(totalCap)}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Occupied</span><span class="value">${formatNumber(occupied)}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Available</span><span class="value">${formatNumber(totalCap - occupied)}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Occupancy</span><span class="value" style="color:${getOccupancyColor(fz.occupancy)}">${fz.occupancy}%</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Barcode</span><span class="value" style="font-size:0.75rem">${fz.barcode}</span></div>`;
            document.getElementById("bbInfoPanel").classList.add("show");
        }

        function showShelfInfo(shelfId, racks, drawers, boxes, tubes, occ) {
            var occupied = Math.floor(tubes * occ / 100);
            document.getElementById("infoIcon").innerHTML = "&#128218;";
            document.getElementById("infoTitle").textContent = shelfId;
            document.getElementById("infoSubtitle").textContent = "Storage Shelf";
            document.getElementById("infoContent").innerHTML =
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Racks</span><span class="value">${racks}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Drawers</span><span class="value">${drawers}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Boxes</span><span class="value">${boxes}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Tube Capacity</span><span class="value">${formatNumber(tubes)}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Occupied</span><span class="value">${formatNumber(occupied)}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Available</span><span class="value">${formatNumber(tubes - occupied)}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Occupancy</span><span class="value" style="color:${getOccupancyColor(occ)}">${occ}%</span></div>`;
            document.getElementById("bbInfoPanel").classList.add("show");
        }

        function showRackInfo(rackId, shelfIdx, occ) {

            document.getElementById("infoTitle").textContent = rackId;
            document.getElementById("infoSubtitle").textContent = "Storage Rack";

        function showDrawerInfo(drawerId, occ) {
            document.getElementById("infoIcon").innerHTML = "&#128448;";
            document.getElementById("infoTitle").textContent = drawerId;
            document.getElementById("infoSubtitle").textContent = "Storage Drawer";
            document.getElementById("infoContent").innerHTML =
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Boxes</span><span class="value">5</span></div>` +

                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Occupancy</span><span class="value" style="color:${getOccupancyColor(occ)}">${occ}%</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Status</span><span class="value" style="color:var(--bb-success)">&#9679; ONLINE</span></div>`;
            document.getElementById("bbInfoPanel").classList.add("show");
        }

        function showBoxInfo(boxId, occ) {
            var occupied = Math.floor(100 * occ / 100);
            document.getElementById("infoIcon").innerHTML = "&#128230;";
            document.getElementById("infoTitle").textContent = boxId;
            document.getElementById("infoSubtitle").textContent = "Cryobox";
            document.getElementById("infoContent").innerHTML =
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Grid</span><span class="value">10 x 10</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Total Positions</span><span class="value">100</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Occupied</span><span class="value">${occupied}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Available</span><span class="value">${100 - occupied}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Occupancy</span><span class="value" style="color:${getOccupancyColor(occ)}">${occ}%</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-info-row"><span class="label">Status</span><span class="value" style="color:var(--bb-success)">&#9679; ACTIVE</span></div>`;
            document.getElementById("bbInfoPanel").classList.add("show");
        }

        function showTubeInfo(tubeId, pos, isOccupied, isReserved, event) {
            var tt = document.getElementById("bbTooltip");
            document.getElementById("ttTitle").textContent = tubeId;
            var status = isOccupied ? "Occupied" : (isReserved ? "Reserved" : "Available");
            var statusColor = isOccupied ? "var(--bb-accent)" : (isReserved ? "var(--bb-warning)" : "var(--bb-success)");
            document.getElementById("ttContent").innerHTML =
                `<div class="bb-tooltip-row"><span>Position</span><span>${pos}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-tooltip-row"><span>Row</span><span>${pos.charAt(0)}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-tooltip-row"><span>Column</span><span>${pos.substring(1)}</span></div>` +
                `<div class="bb-tooltip-row"><span>Status</span><span style="color:${statusColor};font-weight:600">${status}</span></div>`;

            tt.style.left = (event.clientX + 15) + "px";
            tt.style.top = (event.clientY + 15) + "px";
            tt.classList.add("show");
        }

        function hideTooltip() {
            document.getElementById("bbTooltip").classList.remove("show");
        }

        function hideInfoPanel() {
            document.getElementById("bbInfoPanel").classList.remove("show");
        }

        function openModal() {
            document.getElementById("bbModal").classList.add("show");
        }

        function closeModal(e) {
            if (!e || e.target.id === "bbModal") {
                document.getElementById("bbModal").classList.remove("show");
            }
        }

        function handleSearch(query) {
            if (!query || query.length < 2) return;
            query = query.toUpperCase();

            for (var i = 0; i < demoData.freezers.length; i++) {
                var fz = demoData.freezers[i];
                if (fz.id.toUpperCase().indexOf(query) >= 0) {
                    navigateTo("freezer", fz);
                    return;
                }
            }
        }

        function attachEvents() {
            var app = document.getElementById("biobankApp");

            app.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
                var actionEl = e.target.closest("[data-action='open-modal']");
                if (actionEl) { openModal(); return; }

                var navEl = e.target.closest("[data-nav]");
                if (!navEl) return;

                var view = navEl.dataset.nav;
                if (view === "room") { navigateTo("room"); return; }

                var freezer = navEl.dataset.freezerId ? findFreezer(navEl.dataset.freezerId) : (currentPath[0] || null);
                var shelfIndex = navEl.dataset.shelfIndex !== undefined ? parseInt(navEl.dataset.shelfIndex, 10) : undefined;
                var rackIndex = navEl.dataset.rackIndex !== undefined ? parseInt(navEl.dataset.rackIndex, 10) : undefined;
                var drawerIndex = navEl.dataset.drawerIndex !== undefined ? parseInt(navEl.dataset.drawerIndex, 10) : undefined;
                var boxId = navEl.dataset.boxId;
                var boxIndex = navEl.dataset.boxIndex !== undefined ? parseInt(navEl.dataset.boxIndex, 10) : undefined;

                if (view === "freezer") navigateTo("freezer", freezer);
                else if (view === "shelf") navigateTo("shelf", { freezer: freezer, shelfIndex: shelfIndex });
                else if (view === "rack") navigateTo("rack", { freezer: freezer, shelfIndex: shelfIndex, rackIndex: rackIndex });
                else if (view === "drawer") navigateTo("drawer", { freezer: freezer, shelfIndex: shelfIndex, rackIndex: rackIndex, drawerIndex: drawerIndex });
                else if (view === "box") navigateTo("box", { freezer: freezer, shelfIndex: shelfIndex, rackIndex: rackIndex, drawerIndex: drawerIndex, boxId: boxId, boxIndex: boxIndex });
            });

            app.addEventListener("mouseover", function (e) {
                var hoverEl = e.target.closest("[data-hover]");
                if (!hoverEl) return;
                var type = hoverEl.dataset.hover;
                if (type === "freezer") showFreezerInfo(hoverEl.dataset.freezerId);
                else if (type === "shelf") showShelfInfo(hoverEl.dataset.shelfId, +hoverEl.dataset.racks, +hoverEl.dataset.drawers, +hoverEl.dataset.boxes, +hoverEl.dataset.tubes, +hoverEl.dataset.occ);
                else if (type === "rack") showRackInfo(hoverEl.dataset.rackId, +hoverEl.dataset.shelfIndex, +hoverEl.dataset.occ);
                else if (type === "drawer") showDrawerInfo(hoverEl.dataset.drawerId, +hoverEl.dataset.occ);
                else if (type === "box") showBoxInfo(hoverEl.dataset.boxId, +hoverEl.dataset.occ);
                else if (type === "tube") showTubeInfo(hoverEl.dataset.tubeId, hoverEl.dataset.pos, hoverEl.dataset.occupied === "true", hoverEl.dataset.reserved === "true", e);
            });

            app.addEventListener("mouseout", function (e) {
                var hoverEl = e.target.closest("[data-hover]");
                if (!hoverEl) return;
                if (hoverEl.contains(e.relatedTarget)) return;
                if (hoverEl.dataset.hover === "tube") hideTooltip(); else hideInfoPanel();
            });

            var searchBox = document.getElementById("bbSearch");
            if (searchBox) {
                searchBox.addEventListener("input", function () { handleSearch(this.value); });
            }

            var modalOverlay = document.getElementById("bbModal");
            if (modalOverlay) {
                modalOverlay.addEventListener("click", function (e) { closeModal(e); });
            }
            var modalCloseBtn = document.getElementById("bbModalCloseBtn");
            if (modalCloseBtn) {
                modalCloseBtn.addEventListener("click", function () { closeModal(); });
            }
        }

        attachEvents();
        renderRoom();
        updateMinimap();
    </script>
}

<script>
    window.storageCondition = {
        room: @Model.StorageCondition.RoomTempCount,
        freezer: @Model.StorageCondition.FreezerCount,
        cold: @Model.StorageCondition.ColdCount
    };
</script>
@section Scripts {

    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/apexcharts"></script>
    <script src="~/js/warehouse-dashboard.js"></script>

    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

            const counters = document.querySelectorAll(".kpi-number");

            counters.forEach(counter => {
                const target = +counter.getAttribute("data-count");
                let current = 0;
                const increment = Math.max(1, Math.floor(target / 1000));

                const update = () => {
                    current += increment;
                    if (current >= target) {
                        counter.innerText = target;
                    } else {
                        counter.innerText = current;
                        requestAnimationFrame(update);
                    }
                };

                update();
            });

        });
    </script>
    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

            if (typeof ApexCharts === "undefined") {
                console.error("ApexCharts not loaded");
                return;
            }

            const waveData = [
                @foreach (var item in Model.SdsWaveItems)
                {
                        var value = item.IsValid ? item.Quantity : -item.Quantity;
                        <text>{
                            x: "@item.ItemName",
                            y: @value,
                            status: "@(item.IsValid ? "Valid" : "Invalid")"
                        },</text>
                }
            ];

            const options = {
                chart: {
                    type: 'bar',
                    height: 260,
                    toolbar: { show: false },
                    animations: {
                        enabled: true,
                        easing: 'easeinout',
                        speed: 1200,
                        animateGradually: { enabled: true, delay: 40 },
                        dynamicAnimation: { enabled: true, speed: 900 }
                    }
                },

                plotOptions: {
                    bar: {
                        columnWidth: '38%',
                        borderRadius: 6
                    }
                },

                series: [{
                    name: 'SDS Status',
                    data: waveData
                }],

                grid: {
                    strokeDashArray: 4,
                    borderColor: '#e9ecef'
                },

                xaxis: {
                    labels: { show: false },
                    axisBorder: { show: false },
                    axisTicks: { show: false }
                },

                yaxis: {
                    labels: {
                        formatter: val => Math.abs(val)
                    }
                },

                colors: [
                    function ({ value }) {
                        return value >= 0
                            ? 'rgba(32,201,151,0.95)'   // Valid
                            : 'rgba(255,107,107,0.95)'; // Invalid
                    }
                ],

                tooltip: {
                    custom: function ({ seriesIndex, dataPointIndex, w }) {

                        const point = w.config.series[seriesIndex].data[dataPointIndex];
                        const qty = Math.abs(point.y);
                        const isValid = point.status === "Valid";

                        return `
                            <div class="sds-tooltip ${isValid ? 'valid' : 'invalid'}">
                                <div class="sds-title">${point.x}</div>
                                <div class="sds-qty">Quantity: <b>${qty}</b></div>
                                <div class="sds-badge">
                                    <span class="badge ${isValid ? 'bg-success' : 'bg-danger'}">
                                        ${point.status}
                                    </span>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        `;
                    }
                },

                dataLabels: { enabled: false }
            };

            new ApexCharts(
                document.querySelector("#sdsWaveChart"),
                options
            ).render();
        });
    </script>

    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

            const el = document.querySelector("#storageConditionBar");
            if (!el || !window.storageCondition) return;

            const data = [
                storageCondition.room || 0,
                storageCondition.freezer || 0,
                storageCondition.cold || 0
            ];

            // لو مفيش داتا
            if (data.every(x => x === 0)) {
                el.innerHTML = "<p class='text-muted mt-5'>No storage data available</p>";
                return;
            }

            const options = {
                chart: {
                    type: 'bar',
                    height: 260,
                    toolbar: { show: false }
                },
                plotOptions: {
                    bar: {
                        horizontal: true,
                        borderRadius: 10,
                        distributed: true, // 🔥 مهم
                        barHeight: '55%'
                    }
                },
                series: [{
                    name: 'Items',
                    data: data
                }],
                colors: [
                    '#F96E5B', // 🔴 Room Temp
                    '#AEDEFC', // 🔵 Freezer
                    '#3BC1A8'  // 🟢 Cold
                ],
                xaxis: {
                    categories: [
                        'Room Temp',
                        'Freezer °C',
                        '2–8 °C'
                    ]
                },
                dataLabels: {
                    enabled: true,
                    style: {
                        fontSize: '14px',
                        fontWeight: 'bold'
                    }
                },
                tooltip: {
                    y: {
                        formatter: val => val + " Items"
                    }
                }
            };

            new ApexCharts(el, options).render();
        });
    </script>
    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

            var seriesData = @Html.Raw(Json.Serialize(
                            Model.CategorySummary.Select(x => x.ItemsCount)
                    ));

        var labelsData = @Html.Raw(Json.Serialize(
                        Model.CategorySummary.Select(x => x.CategoryName)
                ));

        var options = {
            chart: {
                type: 'pie',
                height: 330,
                animations: {
                    enabled: true,
                    easing: 'easeinout',
                    speed: 900,
                    animateGradually: {
                        enabled: true,
                        delay: 120
                    },
                    dynamicAnimation: {
                        enabled: true,
                        speed: 700
                    }
                }
            },

                series: seriesData,
                labels: labelsData,

                legend: {
                    position: 'bottom',
                    fontSize: '13px',
                    fontWeight: 500,
                    markers: {
                        radius: 12
                    }
                },

                dataLabels: {
                    enabled: true,
                    style: {
                        fontSize: '13px',
                        fontWeight: 'bold'
                    },
                    formatter: function (val, opts) {
                        return opts.w.config.series[opts.seriesIndex];
                    }
                },

                tooltip: {
                    y: {
                        formatter: function (val) {
                            return val + " Items";
                        }
                    }
                },

                colors: [
                    '#FFCB61',
                    '#00809D',
                    '#FF894F',
                    '#7A7A73',
                    '#20c997',
                    
                ],

                stroke: {
                    width: 1,
                    colors: ['#ffffff']
                }
            };

            new ApexCharts(
                document.querySelector("#categoryPieChart"),
                options
            ).render();

        });
    </script>
    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

            const lotsLabels = @Html.Raw(Json.Serialize(Model.LotsOverTime.Select(x => x.Label)));
            const lotsData = @Html.Raw(Json.Serialize(Model.LotsOverTime.Select(x => x.Value)));

            const expiryLabels = @Html.Raw(Json.Serialize(Model.ExpiryTrend.Select(x => x.Label)));
            const expiryData = @Html.Raw(Json.Serialize(Model.ExpiryTrend.Select(x => x.Value)));

            function buildLineChart(el, data, labels, color) {
                return new ApexCharts(document.querySelector(el), {
                    chart: {
                        type: 'line',
                        height: 320,
                        toolbar: { show: false },
                        animations: {
                            enabled: true,
                            easing: 'easeinout',
                            speed: 900
                        }
                    },
                    series: [{
                        name: 'Count',
                        data: data
                    }],
                    xaxis: {
                        categories: labels
                    },
                    stroke: {
                        curve: 'smooth',
                        width: 3
                    },
                    fill: {
                        type: 'gradient',
                        gradient: {
                            shadeIntensity: 1,
                            opacityFrom: 0.4,
                            opacityTo: 0.05,
                            stops: [0, 90, 100]
                        }
                    },
                    colors: [color],
                            markers: {
            size: 7,          // حجم النقطة الأساسي
            strokeWidth: 2,
            strokeColors: '#fff',
            hover: {
                size: 10      // حجمها وقت الـ hover
            }
        },
                    grid: {
                        borderColor: '#eee',
                        strokeDashArray: 4
                    },
                    tooltip: {
                        theme: 'dark',
                        y: {
                            formatter: val => val + " Lots"
                        }
                    }
                });
            }

            buildLineChart(
                "#lotsLineChart",
                lotsData,
                lotsLabels,
                "#198754" // Green
            ).render();

            buildLineChart(
                "#expiryLineChart",
                expiryData,
                expiryLabels,
                "#dc3545" // Red
            ).render();

        });
    </script>
    <script>
        function showPageLoader() {
            document.getElementById("pageLoader").classList.remove("d-none");
        }

        // أمان زيادة – لو رجع المستخدم Back
        window.addEventListener("pageshow", function () {
            document.getElementById("pageLoader")?.classList.add("d-none");
        });
    </script>




}
