@section Scripts{
    <script>
        window.addEventListener("load", () => {
            const input = document.getElementById("lotQnty");

            setTimeout(() => {
                input.focus();
                input.select();
            }, 100);
        });

                const selRoom = document.getElementById('selRoom');
        const selUnit = document.getElementById('selUnit');
        const selShelf = document.getElementById('selShelf');
        const selBox = document.getElementById('selBox');

        function resetSelect(sel, placeholder) {
            sel.innerHTML = `<option value="" selected disabled>${placeholder}</option>`;
            sel.disabled = true;
        }

        function hideGrid() {
            document.getElementById('gridWrapper').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('hiddenBoxId').value = '';
            document.getElementById('hiddenRowIndex').value = '';
            document.getElementById('hiddenColumnIndex').value = '';
        }

        selRoom.addEventListener('change', async () => {
            resetSelect(selUnit, 'Choose Unit');
            resetSelect(selShelf, 'Choose Shelf');
            resetSelect(selBox, 'Choose Box');
            hideGrid();

            const res = await fetch(`/Rooms/GetStorageUnitsByRoom?roomId=${selRoom.value}`);
            const units = await res.json();
            units.forEach(u => selUnit.add(new Option(u.name, u.id)));
            selUnit.disabled = false;
        });

        selUnit.addEventListener('change', async () => {
            resetSelect(selShelf, 'Choose Shelf');
            resetSelect(selBox, 'Choose Box');
            hideGrid();

            const res = await fetch(`/Rooms/GetShelvesByStorageUnit?storageUnitId=${selUnit.value}`);
            const shelves = await res.json();
            shelves.forEach(s => selShelf.add(new Option(s.name, s.id)));
            selShelf.disabled = false;
        });

        selShelf.addEventListener('change', async () => {
            resetSelect(selBox, 'Choose Box');
            hideGrid();

            const res = await fetch(`/Rooms/GetBoxesByShelf?shelfId=${selShelf.value}`);
            const boxes = await res.json();
            boxes.forEach(b => selBox.add(new Option(b.name, b.id)));
            selBox.disabled = false;
        });

        selBox.addEventListener('change', async () => {
            const res = await fetch(`/Rooms/GetBoxGrid?boxId=${selBox.value}`);
            const gridData = await res.json();
            if (!gridData) return;

            renderGrid(gridData);
            document.getElementById('hiddenBoxId').value = selBox.value;
            document.getElementById('gridWrapper').style.display = 'block';
        });

        function renderGrid(data) {
            const table = document.getElementById('boxGridTable');
            table.innerHTML = '';

            const occupiedMap = {};
            data.occupied.forEach(o => {
                occupiedMap[`${o.rowIndex}-${o.columnIndex}`] = o.lotNumber;
            });

            for (let r = 1; r <= data.rowsCount; r++) {
                const tr = document.createElement('tr');
                for (let c = 1; c <= data.columnsCount; c++) {
                    const td = document.createElement('td');
                    const key = `${r}-${c}`;

                    if (occupiedMap[key]) {
                        td.className = 'cell-occupied';
                        td.title = `Occupied by Lot: ${occupiedMap[key]}`;
                        td.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-x-lg" style="font-size:12px;color:#a94442;"></i>';
                    } else {
                        td.className = 'cell-free';
                        td.title = `Row ${r}, Column ${c}`;
                        td.addEventListener('click', () => selectCell(td, r, c));
                    }
                    tr.appendChild(td);
                }
                table.appendChild(tr);
            }
        }

        function selectCell(td, r, c) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.cell-selected').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('cell-selected'));
            td.classList.add('cell-selected');
            td.classList.remove('cell-free');
            td.classList.add('cell-free'); // يفضل قابل لإعادة الاختيار

            document.getElementById('hiddenRowIndex').value = r;
            document.getElementById('hiddenColumnIndex').value = c;
            document.getElementById('selectedSlotLabel').textContent = `Selected: Row ${r}, Column ${c}`;
        }

        // منع الحفظ من غير ما يختار مكان فعلي
        document.querySelector('form').addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
            const boxId = document.getElementById('hiddenBoxId').value;
            const rowIndex = document.getElementById('hiddenRowIndex').value;

            if (!boxId || !rowIndex) {
                e.preventDefault();
                Swal.fire({
                    title: 'Location Required',
                    text: 'Please select a storage slot before saving.',
                    icon: 'warning',
                    confirmButtonText: 'Ok'
                });
            }
        });
    </script>
}
<style>
    .cell-free {
        background: #d4edda;
        border: 1px solid #b7dfc0;
        width: 38px;
        height: 38px;
        cursor: pointer;
        text-align: center;
        vertical-align: middle;
        transition: .15s;
    }

        .cell-free:hover {
            background: #a9dcb5;
            transform: scale(1.05);
        }

    .cell-occupied {
        background: #f5c6cb;
        border: 1px solid #eab5ba;
        width: 38px;
        height: 38px;
        text-align: center;
        vertical-align: middle;
        cursor: not-allowed;
    }

    .cell-selected {
        background: #0d6efd !important;
        color: #fff;
    }

    .legend-box {
        display: inline-block;
        width: 14px;
        height: 14px;
        border-radius: 3px;
        margin-left: 4px;
        vertical-align: middle;
    }

    .legend-free {
        background: #d4edda;
        border: 1px solid #b7dfc0;
    }

    .legend-occupied {
        background: #f5c6cb;
        border: 1px solid #eab5ba;
    }

    .legend-selected {
        background: #0d6efd;
    }
</style>

@inject UserManager<ApplicationUser> userManager
@model ItemViewModel

<!-- Hero Header -->

    <div class="container-fluid">
        <!--  Modern Pill (Recommended) -->
        <nav aria-label="breadcrumb" class="breadcrumb-nav-pill mx-auto text-center justify-content-center" dir="ltr">
            <ol class="breadcrumb-list">
                <li class="breadcrumb-step completed">
                    <a asp-controller="Items" asp-action="Index" class="step-link">
                        <span class="step-icon"><i class="bi bi-archive-fill"></i></span>
                        <span class="step-label">Items</span>

                    <span class="step-arrow"><i class="bi bi-chevron-right"></i></span>

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        window.addEventListener("load", () => {
            const input = document.getElementById("lotExQnty");

            setTimeout(() => {
                input.focus();
                input.select();
            }, 100);
        });
    </script>
    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
            const lotList = document.getElementById("lotList");
            const lotBarcode = document.getElementById("lotBarcode");
            const lotNumber = document.getElementById("lotNumber");
            const lotNum = document.getElementById("lotNum");
            const lotExQnty = document.getElementById("lotExQnty");
            const lotId = document.getElementById("lotId");


        function updateLotDetails() {
            const selectedOption = lotList.options[lotList.selectedIndex];
            const barcode = selectedOption.dataset.barcode;
            const code = selectedOption.dataset.code;
            const available = parseInt(selectedOption.dataset.available);
            const id = parseInt(selectedOption.dataset.id);

            // تحديث صورة الباركود ورقم اللوت
            lotBarcode.src = barcode;
            lotNumber.textContent = code;
            lotNum.value = code;
            lotExQnty.max = available;
            lotExQnty.value = "";
            lotExQnty.placeholder = "Max Quantity : " + available;
            lotId.value = id;
        }
            lotList.addEventListener("change", updateLotDetails);
            updateLotDetails();

        });
    </script>
}

    @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Context.Session.GetString(Helper.MsgType)))
    {

        {

                Swal.fire({
                title: '@Context.Session.GetString(Helper.Title)',
                text: '@Html.Raw(@Context.Session.GetString(Helper.Msg))',
                confirmButtonText: 'Ok',
                icon: 'success'
                });
            </script>

        }
        else
        {
            <script>

                Swal.fire({
                title: '@Context.Session.GetString(Helper.Title)',
                text: '@Html.Raw(@Context.Session.GetString(Helper.Msg))',
                confirmButtonText: 'Ok',
                icon: 'error'
                });
            </script>
        }
        Context.Session.SetString(Helper.MsgType, "");
    }

    <script>

        let lbTitleEdit = '@Html.Raw("Edit Item")';

    int tNo = 0;
}

<div class="lot-hero" dir="ltr">

                    </div>
                    <span class="header-title" style="color:#bf360c;">Item Lots</span>
                </div>
                <div class="card-body-lot">
                    @if (!Model.LotsList.Any())
                    {
                        <div class="tx-empty">
                            <i class="bi bi-inbox"></i>
                            <h5>No Lots in this Item</h5>
                            <p class="text-muted small mb-0">No lots have been added to this item yet.</p>
                        </div>

                    else
                    {
                        <div class="table-responsive">
                            <table class="tx-table">
                                <thead>
                                    <tr>
                                        <th>Barcode</th>
                                        <th>Expiry Date</th>
                                        <th>Available Qty</th>
                                        <th>Lot SDS</th>
                                        <th>Manufacture Date</th>
                                        <th></th>
                                    </tr>
                                </thead>
                                <tbody>
                                    @foreach (var lot in Model.LotsList)
                                    {
                                        int daysLeft = (lot.ExpiryDate - DateTime.Now.Date).Value.Days;
                                        if (daysLeft <= 0)
                                        {
                                            badgeClass = "bg-danger text-white";
                                            badgeText = "Expired";
                                        }
                                        else if (daysLeft <= 30)
                                        {
                                            badgeClass = "bg-warning text-dark";
                                            badgeText = "Near Expiry";
                                        }
                                        else
                                        {
                                            badgeClass = "bg-success text-white";
                                            badgeText = "Valid";
                                        }
                                        <tr>
                                            <td>
                                                <span class="badge @badgeClass rounded-pill w-100 mb-1">@badgeText</span>
                                                <img src="@lot.BarcodeImage" class="barcode-img rounded-2 shadow-sm bg-white p-1 w-100" />
                                                <div style="font-size:11px" class="text-muted">@lot.LotNumber</div>
                                            </td>
                                            <td class="fw-bold">@Convert.ToDateTime(lot.ExpiryDate).ToString("yyyy-MM-dd")</td>
                                            <td class="fw-bold text-primary">@lot.AvilableQuantity</td>
                                            <td>
                                                @if (lot.SDS == true)
                                                {
                                                    <span class="tx-badge in"><i class="bi bi-check-circle-fill"></i>Valid</span>
                                                }
                                                else
                                                {
                                                    <span class="tx-badge out"><i class="bi bi-x-circle-fill"></i>Invalid</span>
                                                }
                                            </td>
                                            <td>@Convert.ToDateTime(lot.ManufactureDate).ToString("yyyy-MM-dd")</td>
                                            <td>
                                                @if (AuthorizationService.AuthorizeAsync(User, Permissions.Lots.Details_Lots).Result.Succeeded)
                                                {
                                                    <a class="btn-icon text-dark" title="Details" asp-controller="Lots" asp-action="SelectedLot" asp-route-Id="@lot.Id">
                                                        <i class="bi bi-eye-fill"></i>
                                                    </a>
                                                }
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                    }
                                </tbody>
                            </table>
                        </div>
                    }
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Expired Lots -->
            <div class="lot-card mt-4">
                <div class="card-header-lot" style="background: linear-gradient(135deg, #ffebee 0%, #ffcdd2 100%);">
                    <div class="header-icon" style="background: var(--danger);">
                        <i class="bi bi-calendar-x-fill" style="color:#fff;"></i>
                    </div>
                    <span class="header-title" style="color:#c62828;">Expired Lots</span>
                </div>
                <div class="card-body-lot">
                    @if (!Model.ExpieredLots.Any())
                    {
                        <div class="tx-empty">
                            <i class="bi bi-inbox"></i>
                            <h5>No Expired Lots</h5>
                            <p class="text-muted small mb-0">No expired lots have been added to this item yet.</p>

                    {

                            <table class="tx-table">
                                <thead>
                                    <tr>

                                            </td>

                                                    <span class="tx-badge out"><i class="bi bi-x-circle-fill"></i>Invalid</span>
                                                }
                                            </td>
                                            <td>@Convert.ToDateTime(lot.ManufactureDate).ToString("yyyy-MM-dd")</td>
                                            <td>
                                                @if (AuthorizationService.AuthorizeAsync(User, Permissions.Lots.Details_Lots).Result.Succeeded)
                                                {
                                                    <a class="btn-icon text-dark" title="Details" asp-controller="Lots" asp-action="SelectedLot" asp-route-Id="@lot.Id">
                                                        <i class="bi bi-eye-fill"></i>

    <!-- Storage Locations Section -->
    <div class="row mt-4">
        <div class="col-12">
            <div class="lot-card">
                <div class="card-header-lot" style="background: linear-gradient(135deg, #e8f5e9 0%, #c8e6c9 100%);">
                    <div class="header-icon" style="background: var(--success);">
                        <i class="bi bi-geo-alt-fill" style="color: #fff;"></i>
                    </div>
                    <span class="header-title" style="color: #2e7d32;">Storage Locations</span>
                </div>
                <div class="card-body-lot">
                    @{
                        var activeLots = Model.LotsList.Where(l => l.AvilableQuantity > 0).ToList();
                        var boxGroups = activeLots.Where(l => l.Box != null).GroupBy(l => l.Box.Id).ToList();
                        var noBoxLots = activeLots.Where(l => l.Box == null).ToList();
                    }
                    @if (!activeLots.Any())
                    {
                        <div class="tx-empty">
                            <i class="bi bi-inbox"></i>
                            <h5>No Active Storage Locations</h5>
                            <p class="text-muted small mb-0">No lots with available quantity for this item.</p>
                        </div>
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        <div class="accordion" id="storageAccordion">
                            @{
                                int sIdx = 0;
                            }
                            @foreach (var grp in boxGroups)
                            {
                                sIdx++;
                                var collapseId = "storageCollapse" + sIdx;
                                var box = grp.First().Box;
                                var lotsInBox = grp.ToList();
                                <div class="accordion-item mb-3" style="border-radius:12px; overflow:hidden; border:1.5px solid var(--border);">
                                    <h2 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button class="accordion-button @(sIdx == 1 ? "" : "collapsed")" type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#@collapseId">
                                            <i class="bi bi-grid-3x3 me-2"></i>
                                            <span class="fw-bold me-2">Box: @box.Name</span>
                                            <span class="badge bg-primary-subtle text-primary me-2">@lotsInBox.Count Lot(s)</span>
                                            <span class="text-muted small">@(box?.Shelf?.StorageUnit?.Room?.Name ?? "N/A") / @(box?.Shelf?.StorageUnit?.Name ?? "N/A") / @(box?.Shelf?.Name ?? "N/A")</span>
                                        </button>
                                    </h2>
                                    <div id="@collapseId" class="accordion-collapse collapse @(sIdx == 1 ? "show" : "")" data-bs-parent="#storageAccordion">
                                        <div class="accordion-body d-flex gap-3 flex-wrap">
                                            <div class="storage-visual col-md-6 col-12">
                                                <div class="storage-path">
                                                    <!-- Room -->
                                                    <div class="storage-node">
                                                        <div class="node-icon building">
                                                            <i class="bi bi-building"></i>
                                                        </div>
                                                        <div class="node-content">
                                                            <div class="node-label">Room</div>
                                                            <div class="node-value">@(box?.Shelf?.StorageUnit?.Room?.Name ?? "N/A")</div>
                                                        </div>
                                                    </div>
                                                    <div class="node-arrow"><i class="bi bi-chevron-down"></i></div>

                                                    <!-- Storage Unit -->
                                                    <div class="storage-node">
                                                        <div class="node-icon unit">
                                                            <i class="bi bi-box-seam"></i>
                                                        </div>
                                                        <div class="node-content">
                                                            <div class="node-label">Storage Unit</div>
                                                            <div class="node-value">@(box?.Shelf?.StorageUnit?.Name ?? "N/A")</div>
                                                        </div>
                                                    </div>
                                                    <div class="node-arrow"><i class="bi bi-chevron-down"></i></div>

                                                    <!-- Shelf -->
                                                    <div class="storage-node">
                                                        <div class="node-icon shelf">
                                                            <i class="bi bi-layers"></i>
                                                        </div>
                                                        <div class="node-content">
                                                            <div class="node-label">Shelf</div>
                                                            <div class="node-value">@(box?.Shelf?.Name ?? "N/A")</div>
                                                        </div>
                                                    </div>
                                                    <div class="node-arrow"><i class="bi bi-chevron-down"></i></div>

                                                    <!-- Box -->
                                                    <div class="storage-node">
                                                        <div class="node-icon box">
                                                            <i class="bi bi-grid-3x3"></i>
                                                        </div>
                                                        <div class="node-content">
                                                            <div class="node-label">Box</div>
                                                            <div class="node-value">@(box?.Name ?? "N/A")</div>
                                                            <div class="text-muted small">@box?.RowsCount × @box?.ColumnsCount grid</div>
                                                        </div>
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>

                                                <!-- Lots in this box list -->
                                                <div class="mt-3">
                                                    <span class="fw-bold text-muted small"><i class="bi bi-list-ul me-1"></i>Lots in this Box</span>
                                                    @foreach (var lt in lotsInBox)
                                                    {
                                                        <div class="info-row">
                                                            <span class="info-label"><i class="bi bi-upc-scan"></i> @lt.LotNumber</span>
                                                            <span class="info-value">Qty: @lt.AvilableQuantity &nbsp;|&nbsp; Exp: @Convert.ToDateTime(lt.ExpiryDate).ToString("yyyy-MM-dd")</span>
                                                        </div>
                                                    }
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                            <div class="storage-visual col-md-5 col-12">
                                                <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-2">
                                                    <span class="fw-bold text-muted small"><i class="bi bi-grid-3x3 me-1"></i>Box Grid View</span>
                                                    <span class="badge bg-primary-subtle text-primary small">
                                                        <i class="bi bi-geo-fill me-1"></i>Hover a slot for details
                                                    </span>
                                                </div>
                                                <div class="slot-grid-visual" style="grid-template-columns: repeat(@box.ColumnsCount, 1fr);">
                                                    @for (int r = 1; r <= box.RowsCount; r++)
                                                    {
                                                        @for (int c = 1; c <= box.ColumnsCount; c++)
                                                        {
                                                            var occupant = lotsInBox.FirstOrDefault(l => l.RowIndex == r && l.ColumnIndex == c);
                                                            if (occupant != null)
                                                            {
                                                                <div class="slot-cell current" data-bs-toggle="popover" data-bs-html="true" data-bs-trigger="hover focus" data-bs-placement="top"
                                                                     data-bs-content='<div class="text-center"><img src="@occupant.BarcodeImage" height="32" class="mb-1" /><br /><b>@occupant.LotNumber</b><br />Qty: @occupant.AvilableQuantity<br />Exp: @Convert.ToDateTime(occupant.ExpiryDate).ToString("yyyy-MM-dd")</div>'>
                                                                    <i class="bi bi-geo-fill"></i>
                                                                </div>
                                                            }
                                                            else
                                                            {
                                                                <div class="slot-cell empty" title="Row @(r), Col @(c)">
                                                                    <span>@(r),@(c)</span>
                                                                </div>
                                                            }
                                                        }
                                                    }
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            }

                            @if (noBoxLots.Any())
                            {
                                sIdx++;
                                var collapseId = "storageCollapse" + sIdx;
                                <div class="accordion-item mb-3" style="border-radius:12px; overflow:hidden; border:1.5px solid var(--border);">
                                    <h2 class="accordion-header">
                                        <button class="accordion-button collapsed" type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse" data-bs-target="#@collapseId">
                                            <i class="bi bi-question-diamond me-2"></i>
                                            <span class="fw-bold me-2">Unassigned Location</span>
                                            <span class="badge bg-secondary-subtle text-secondary me-2">@noBoxLots.Count Lot(s)</span>
                                        </button>
                                    </h2>
                                    <div id="@collapseId" class="accordion-collapse collapse" data-bs-parent="#storageAccordion">
                                        <div class="accordion-body">
                                            @foreach (var lt in noBoxLots)
                                            {
                                                <div class="info-row">
                                                    <span class="info-label"><i class="bi bi-upc-scan"></i> @lt.LotNumber</span>
                                                    <span class="info-value">Qty: @lt.AvilableQuantity &nbsp;|&nbsp; Exp: @Convert.ToDateTime(lt.ExpiryDate).ToString("yyyy-MM-dd")</span>
                                                </div>
                                            }
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            }
                        </div>
                    }
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Transactions Section -->
    <div class="row mt-4">
        <div class="col-12">
            <div class="lot-card">
                <div class="card-header-lot" style="background: linear-gradient(135deg, #eceff1 0%, #cfd8dc 100%);">
                    <div class="header-icon" style="background:#37474f;">
                        <i class="bi bi-arrow-left-right" style="color:#fff;"></i>
                    </div>
                    <span class="header-title" style="color:#263238;">Item Transactions</span>
                </div>
                <div class="card-body-lot">
                    @if (!Model.ItemTransactionsList.Any())
                    {
                        <div class="tx-empty">
                            <i class="bi bi-inbox"></i>
                            <h5>No Transactions Yet</h5>
                            <p class="text-muted small mb-0">This item has no transaction history.</p>
                        </div>
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        <div class="table-responsive">
                            <table class="tx-table">
                                <thead>
                                    <tr>
                                        <th>#</th>
                                        <th>Date</th>
                                        <th>Statement</th>
                                        <th>Created By</th>
                                        <th>Device</th>
                                        <th>Qty</th>
                                        <th>Available</th>
                                    </tr>
                                </thead>
                                <tbody>
                                    @foreach (var trans in Model.ItemTransactionsList)
                                    {
                                        tNo++;
                                        <tr>
                                            <td class="fw-bold text-muted">@trans.Move_Num</td>
                                            <td>@Convert.ToDateTime(trans.Move_Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-dd")</td>
                                            <td>@trans.Move_Statement</td>
                                            <td><i class="bi bi-person-circle me-1 text-muted"></i>@trans.CreatedBy</td>
                                            <td><i class="bi bi-laptop me-1 text-muted"></i>@trans.DeviceUsing</td>
                                            <td>
                                                @if (trans.Move_State == true)
                                                {
                                                    <span class="tx-badge in"><i class="bi bi-arrow-up-circle-fill"></i>+@trans.Move_Quantity</span>
                                                }
                                                else
                                                {
                                                    <span class="tx-badge out"><i class="bi bi-arrow-down-circle-fill"></i>-@trans.Move_Quantity</span>
                                                }
                                            </td>
                                            <td class="fw-bold text-primary">@trans.Total_Quantity</td>
                                        </tr>
                                    }
                                </tbody>
                            </table>
                        </div>
                    }
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
            var popoverTriggerList = [].slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-bs-toggle="popover"]'));
            popoverTriggerList.forEach(function (el) {
                new bootstrap.Popover(el);
            });
        });
    </script>
}




========================= Locations ==================


@model LocationViewModel


<!-- Modal -->
<div class="modal fade " id="exampleModal" tabindex="-1" aria-labelledby="exampleModalLabel" aria-hidden="true" dir="ltr">
<div class="modal-dialog card card-success card-outline">
    <div class="modal-content">
        <div class="modal-header" style="background-color:#758A93" dir="ltr">
            <span class="fs-4 fw-bold modal-title" id="title"></span>
            <a href="#" class="btn-close position-absolute  end-0 mx-3" data-bs-dismiss="modal" aria-label="Close"></a>
        </div>
            <div class="modal-body">
                <form asp-action="Save" asp-controller="Locations" method="post">
                    <input type="text" hidden class="form-control" asp-for="NewLocation.Id" id="locId" value="0" />
                    <!-- LocationName -->
                    <div class="input-group mt-2 row mx-auto">
                        <span class="input-group-text bg-info-subtle fw-bold rounded-end-3 col-md-4" id="inputGroup-sizing-default ">Building</span>
                        <input id="locName"  readonly value="Main Storage" asp-for="NewLocation.LocationName" class="form-control text-center" autocomplete="on" aria-required="true" placeholder="Enter Building Name ..." type="text" aria-label="Sizing example input" aria-describedby="inputGroup-sizing-default" style="border-right-color:red;border-top-right-radius : 20px ;">
                    </div>
                    <span asp-validation-for="NewLocation.LocationName" class="text-danger"></span>

        {

                Swal.fire({
                    title: '@Context.Session.GetString(Helper.Title)',
                    text: '@Html.Raw(@Context.Session.GetString(Helper.Msg))',
                    confirmButtonText: 'Ok',
                    icon: 'success'
                });
            </script>

        }
        else
        {
            <script>

                Swal.fire({
                    title: '@Context.Session.GetString(Helper.Title)',
                    text: '@Html.Raw(@Context.Session.GetString(Helper.Msg))',
                    confirmButtonText: 'Ok',
                    icon: 'error'
                });
            </script>
        }
        Context.Session.SetString(Helper.MsgType, "");
    }

                                        @if (item.SDS == true)
                                        {
                                            <span class="py-1 px-3 bg-success-subtle link-success rounded-5">
                                                Valid
                                            </span>
                                        }
                                        else
                                        {
                                            <span class="py-1 px-3 bg-danger-subtle link-danger rounded-5">
                                                Invalid
                                            </span>
                                        }
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="ps-2"><strong>Available Quantity:</strong> <span class="rounded-4 bg-warning-subtle fw-bold px-3 py-1 text-dark">@item.AvilableQuantity</span></div>

                                </div>

                                <div class="item-actions mt-0 mb-1">
                                    @if (canExchange)
                                    {
                                        <a asp-controller="Items" asp-action="ExchangeLot" asp-route-id="@item.Id"
                                           class="btn action-btn" data-action="exchange" title="Exchange Lot">
                                            <i class="bi bi-cart-dash-fill fs-1"></i>
                                        </a>
                                    }
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        }
                    </div>
                }
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        (function() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('ListItemsModalExchange');
            if (!modal) return;

            const input = modal.querySelector('#itemsSearchInputExchange');
            const cards = modal.querySelectorAll('.item-card');
            const scanMsg = modal.querySelector('#scanMessageExchange');

            let timer;
            input.addEventListener('input', () => {
                clearTimeout(timer);
                timer = setTimeout(() => {
                    const code = input.value.trim();
                    input.value = '';
                    scanMsg.classList.add('d-none');
                    const match = [...cards].find(c => c.dataset.barcode === code);
                    if (!match) return scanMsg.classList.remove('d-none');
                    const btn = match.querySelector('.action-btn[data-action="exchange"]');
                    if (btn) btn.click();
                }, 300);
            });

            modal.addEventListener('shown.bs.modal', () => input.focus());
        })();
    </script>
}

                                                badgeClass = "bg-warning text-dark";
                                                badgeText = "Near Expiry";
                                            }
                                            else
                                            {
                                                borderColor = "#198754"; // success
                                                badgeClass = "bg-success text-white";
                                                badgeText = "Valid";
                                            }

                                            <!-- STATUS BADGE -->
                                            
                                                <tr class="text-center ">

                                                    <td class="text-center">
                                                   
                                                        <span class="badge @badgeClass rounded-pill w-100">
                                                            @badgeText
                                                        </span>
                                                        <img src="@lot.BarcodeImage"
                                                             class="barcode-img rounded-2 shadow-sm bg-white p-1 w-100" />
                                                        <div style="font-size:12px" class="text-dark">
                                                            @lot.LotNumber
                                                        </div>
                                                    </td>
                                                    <td class="fs-6 text-center px-1">
                                                        @if (Model.CanViewItemDetails)
                                                        {
                                                            <a class="link-primary fw-bold text-decoration-none" title="Item Details" asp-controller="Items" asp-action="SelectedItem" asp-route-Id="@lot.ItemId">
                                                                @lot.Item?.Name
                                                            </a>
                                                        }
                                                        else
                                                        {
                                                            <span class="link-primary fw-bold text-decoration-none" title="Item Details">
                                                                @lot.Item?.Name
                                                            </span>
                                                        }


                                                    </td>
                                                    <td class="fs-6 text-center px-1 "><span class="fw-bold link-dark">@Convert.ToDateTime(lot.ExpiryDate).ToShortDateString()</span></td>
                                                    <td class="fs-6 text-center px-1 "><span class="link-dark fw-bold">@lot.AvilableQuantity</span></td>
                                                    @* <td class="fs-6 text-center px-1"><span class="link-dark fw-bold">@lot.SupplierLots.FirstOrDefault()</span></td> *@
                                                <td class="fs-6 text-center px-1 "><span class="link-dark fw-bold">@lot.ShortLocation</span></td>
                                                @if (lot.SDS == true)
                                                    {
                                                        <td class="text-center">
                                                            <span class="fw-bold py-2 px-3 bg-success-subtle link-success rounded-5">
                                                                Valid
                                                            </span>
                                                        </td>
                                                    }
                                                    else
                                                    {
                                                        <td class="text-center">
                                                            <span class="fw-bold py-2 px-3 bg-danger-subtle link-danger rounded-5">
                                                                Invalid
                                                            </span>
                                                        </td>
                                                    }

                                                    <td class="fs-6 text-center px-1 "><span class="link-dark ">@Convert.ToDateTime(lot.ReceivedDate).ToShortDateString()</span></td>
@*                                                     <td class="fs-6 text-center px-1 "><span class="link-dark ">@Convert.ToDateTime(lot.ManufactureDate).ToShortDateString()</span></td> *@
                                                <td class="text-start px-1 py-0">
                                                    <span class="link-dark fw-bold px-3">
                                                        <i class="bi bi-thermometer-half @(lot.Item?.StorageUnitType?.TemperatureValue < 0 ? "link-primary" : "link-success") fs-3 px-1"></i>
                                                        @lot.Item?.StorageUnitType?.Name
                                                    </span>
                                                </td>

                                                    <td class="py-1">

                                                        @if (Model.CanViewDetails)
                                                        {

                                                            </span>
                                                        }

                                                        }
                                                        @if (Model.CanPrintBarcode)
                                                        {

@section Scripts {
    <script src="~/js/Users.js"></script>
    @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Context.Session.GetString(Helper.MsgType)))
    {
        if (Context.Session.GetString(Helper.MsgType) == Helper.Success)
        {
            <script>

                Swal.fire({
                    title: '@Context.Session.GetString(Helper.Title)',
                    text: '@Html.Raw(@Context.Session.GetString(Helper.Msg))',
                    confirmButtonText: 'Ok',
                    icon: 'success'
                });
            </script>

        }
        else
        {
            <script>

                Swal.fire({
                    title: '@Context.Session.GetString(Helper.Title)',
                    text: '@Html.Raw(@Context.Session.GetString(Helper.Msg))',
                    confirmButtonText: 'Ok',
                    icon: 'error'
                });
            </script>
        }
        Context.Session.SetString(Helper.MsgType, "");
    }

    <script>

        let lbTitleEdit = '@Html.Raw("Edit Lot")';
        let lbEdit = '@Html.Raw("Save Changes")';
        let lbAddNewRole = '@Html.Raw("Add New Lot")';
        let lbbtnSave = '@Html.Raw("Save Data")';

        let lbTitleMsgDelete = '@Html.Raw("Delete Lot")';
        let lbTextMsgDelete = '@Html.Raw($"Are you sure !  Do you want to delete the Lot data and everything related to it, including requests and records? ")';
        let lbconfirmButtonText = '@Html.Raw("Yes , Remove")';
        let lbcancelButtonText = '@Html.Raw("Cancle")';

        let lbTitleDeletedOk = '@Html.Raw("Delete Lot Done")';
        let lbMsgDeletedOkRole = '@Html.Raw("The Lot data has been successfully deleted!")';
        let lbSuccess = '@Html.Raw(Helper.Success)';

    </script>

    <script>
        (function () {

            const modal = document.getElementById('ListItemsModalExchange');
            if (!modal) return;

            const input = modal.querySelector('#itemsSearchInputExchange');
            const cards = modal.querySelectorAll('.item-card');
            const scanMsg = modal.querySelector('#scanMessageExchange');

            let scanTimer;

            input.addEventListener('input', function () {
                clearTimeout(scanTimer);

                scanTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                    const value = input.value.trim();
                    handleScan(value);
                    input.value = '';
                }, 300);
            });

            function handleScan(code) {
                if (!code) return;

                scanMsg.classList.add('d-none');

                const matches = [...cards].filter(card =>
                    card.dataset.barcode === code
                );

                if (matches.length === 0) {
                    scanMsg.classList.remove('d-none');
                    return;
                }

                if (matches.length === 1) {
                    const btn = matches[0].querySelector(
                        '.action-btn[data-action="exchange"]'
                    );
                    if (btn) btn.click();
                }
            }

            modal.addEventListener('shown.bs.modal', () => {
                input.focus();
            });

        })();
    </script>


    <script>
        (function() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('ListItemsModalCreate');
            if (!modal) return;

            const input = modal.querySelector('#itemsSearchInputCreate');
            const cards = modal.querySelectorAll('.item-card');
            const scanMsg = modal.querySelector('#scanMessageCreate');

            let timer;
            input.addEventListener('input', () => {
                clearTimeout(timer);
                timer = setTimeout(() => {
                    const code = input.value.trim();
                    input.value = '';
                    scanMsg.classList.add('d-none');
                    const match = [...cards].find(c => c.dataset.barcode === code);
                    if (!match) return scanMsg.classList.remove('d-none');
                    const btn = match.querySelector('.action-btn[data-action="create"]');
                    if (btn) btn.click();
                }, 300);
            });

            modal.addEventListener('shown.bs.modal', () => input.focus());
        })();
    </script>
}

@{

<input type="text"
       id="barcodeInput"
       class="form-control"
       placeholder="Scan Item Barcode"
       autofocus />

<select id="lotSelect"
        class="form-control mt-3"
        disabled>
    <option value="">-- اختر Lot --</option>
</select>

<input type="number"
       id="qtyInput"
       class="form-control mt-3"
       placeholder="أدخل الكمية"
       min="1"
       disabled />

<button id="submitBtn"
        class="btn btn-primary mt-3"
        disabled>
    حفظ
</button>

<script>
    let maxQty = 0;

    // Scan Barcode
    document.getElementById("barcodeInput").addEventListener("change", function () {
        fetch(`/Lots/GetLotsByBarcode?barcode=${this.value}`)
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                let select = document.getElementById("lotSelect");
                select.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- اختر Lot --</option>';
                select.disabled = false;

                data.forEach(lot => {
                    let opt = document.createElement("option");
                    opt.value = lot.id;
                    opt.text = lot.displayText;
                    opt.dataset.qty = lot.availableQty;
                    select.appendChild(opt);
                });
            });

        this.value = "";
    });

    // Select Lot
    document.getElementById("lotSelect").addEventListener("change", function () {
        let opt = this.options[this.selectedIndex];
        maxQty = opt.dataset.qty;

        let qty = document.getElementById("qtyInput");
        qty.disabled = false;
        qty.max = maxQty;
        qty.value = "";
        qty.placeholder = `Max: ${maxQty}`;
    });

    // Quantity
    document.getElementById("qtyInput").addEventListener("input", function () {
        if (this.value > maxQty)
            this.value = maxQty;

        document.getElementById("submitBtn").disabled = !this.value;
    });
</script>

@model PermissionsFormViewModel
@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Manage Role Permissions";
}

<div class="card border-0 py-3" style="background-color:#032b42 !important; border-radius:0 0 75px 0 " dir="ltr">
    <div class="d-flex text-center mx-auto ">
        <a asp-controller="Roles" asp-action="index" class="text-center fs-4 fw-bold text-decoration-none" style="color:#415E72 !important ">Roles</a>
        <i class="bi bi-chevron-double-right fw-bold fs-3 mt-2 mx-3" style="color:#415E72 !important"></i>
        <a class="text-center fs-1 fw-bold text-decoration-none" style="color:#415E72 !important ">@ViewData["Title"]</a>
    </div>
</div>

<section class="container-fluid my-3" dir="ltr">
    <form asp-action="ManagePermissions" method="post">

        <!-- Header Card -->
        <div class="card col-md-10 p-0 mx-auto justify-content-center mt-4">
            <div class="card-header d-flex bg-info-subtle py-2 justify-content-start position-relative">
                <div class="px-5">
                    <h2 class="text-dark">@Model.RoleName</h2>
                    <h4 class="text-dark">@Model.Details</h4>
                    <span class="text-dark">Add / Delete Role Permissions</span>
                </div>

                <button asp-route="@Model"
                        type="submit"
                        title="Save Permissons"
                        class="btn btn-info fs-5 btn-sm rounded-3 px-3 position-absolute end-0 mt-3 me-3">
                    <i class="bi bi-bookmark-check-fill fs-3 fw-bold"></i><br />
                    Save Permissions Changes
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>



        <!-- Search -->
        <div class="mt-2 col-md-10 mx-auto mb-3">
            <input type="text"
                   id="permissionSearch"
                   class="form-control form-control-lg"
                   placeholder="Search by Module or Permission..." />
        </div>
        <!-- Modules Cards -->
        <div class="col-md-10 mx-auto mt-4">
            @{
                var groupedModules = Model.RoleCalims
                .Select((claim, index) => new { claim, index })
                .GroupBy(x => x.claim.DisplayValue.Split('.')[1]);
            }
            <div class="row my-3">
            @foreach (var module in groupedModules)
            {
                
                    <div class="col-md-4">
                        <div class="card mb-4 shadow-sm permission-card">
                            <div class="card-header text-white fs-5 fw-bold"
                                 style="background-color:#032b42 !important;">
                                <i class="bi bi-pin-angle-fill  fs-4 pe-3"></i>
                                @module.Key
                            </div>

                            <div class="card-body p-0">
                                <table class="table table-hover m-0 table-bordered border-primary-subtle">
                                    <thead>
                                        <tr class="text-center">
                                            <th class="fs-5 fw-bold bg-info-subtle">Permission</th>
                                            <th class="fs-5 fw-bold bg-info-subtle">Action</th>
                                        </tr>
                                    </thead>
                                    <tbody>
                                        @foreach (var item in module)
                                        {
                                            <tr class="permission-row">
                                                <td class="text-center fs-6">
                                                    @item.claim.DisplayValue.Split('.')[2]
                                                </td>
                                                <td class="text-center">
                                                    <input hidden asp-for="@Model.RoleCalims[item.index].DisplayValue" />
                                                    <input type="checkbox"
                                                           asp-for="@Model.RoleCalims[item.index].IsSelected"
                                                           class="form-check-input border-1 border-primary fs-5" />
                                                </td>
                                            </tr>
                                        }
                                    </tbody>
                                </table>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                
                
            }
            </div>
        </div>

    </form>
</section>

@section Scripts {
    <script src="~/js/Roles.js"></script>
}

<!-- Template Row (Hidden) -->
<template id="unitRowTemplate">
    <div class="unit-row" data-index="__INDEX__">
        <div class="row-header">
            <div class="row-number">__INDEX__</div>
            <span class="row-title">Storage Unit Configuration</span>
        </div>
        <span class="remove-unit-btn" title="Remove this unit type"><i class="bi bi-x-lg"></i></span>

        <div class="row g-3">
            <div class="col-md-5">
                <label class="form-label-custom">
                    <i class="bi bi-box"></i> Storage Unit Type
                </label>
                <select name="Units[__INDEX__].StorageUnitTypeId" class="form-select form-select-custom unit-type-select" required>
                    <option value="" selected disabled>Choose Type...</option>
                    @foreach (var type in Model.ListStorageUnitTypes)
                    {
                        <option value="@type.Value">@type.Text</option>
                    }
                </select>
            </div>
            <div class="col-md-3">
                <label class="form-label-custom">
                    <i class="bi bi-hash"></i> Quantity
                </label>
                <input type="number" min="1" value="1" name="Units[__INDEX__].UnitsCount"
                       class="form-control form-control-custom units-count" required>
            </div>
            <div class="col-md-4 d-flex align-items-end">
                <div class="hazard-check w-100">
                    <input class="form-check-input" type="checkbox" value="true"
                           name="Units[__INDEX__].IsHazardous" id="haz___INDEX__">
                    <input type="hidden" name="Units[__INDEX__].IsHazardous" value="false">
                    <label for="haz___INDEX__">
                        <i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle-fill me-1"></i> Hazardous Material
                    </label>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="col-md-3">
                <label class="form-label-custom">
                    <i class="bi bi-layers"></i> Shelves / Unit
                </label>
                <input type="number" min="1" value="1" name="Units[__INDEX__].ShelvesPerUnit"
                       class="form-control form-control-custom shelves-count" required>
            </div>
            <div class="col-md-3">
                <label class="form-label-custom">
                    <i class="bi bi-grid"></i> Boxes / Shelf
                </label>
                <input type="number" min="1" value="1" name="Units[__INDEX__].BoxesPerShelf"
                       class="form-control form-control-custom boxes-count" required>
            </div>
            <div class="col-md-3">
                <label class="form-label-custom">
                    <i class="bi bi-layout-three-columns"></i> Rows / Box
                </label>
                <input type="number" min="1" value="9" name="Units[__INDEX__].RowsCount"
                       class="form-control form-control-custom rows-count" required>
            </div>
            <div class="col-md-3">
                <label class="form-label-custom">
                    <i class="bi bi-layout-three-columns" style="transform: rotate(90deg); display: inline-block;"></i> Cols / Box
                </label>
                <input type="number" min="1" value="9" name="Units[__INDEX__].ColumnsCount"
                       class="form-control form-control-custom cols-count" required>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</template>

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        let unitIndex = 0;
        const unitsContainer = document.getElementById('unitsContainer');
        const template = document.getElementById('unitRowTemplate').innerHTML;
        const generateBtn = document.getElementById('generateBtn');

        function addUnitRow() {
            const html = template.replaceAll('__INDEX__', unitIndex);
            const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
            wrapper.innerHTML = html;
            const row = wrapper.firstElementChild;
            unitsContainer.appendChild(row);

            // Animate in
            row.style.opacity = '0';
            row.style.transform = 'translateY(20px)';
            requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                row.style.transition = 'all 0.3s ease';
                row.style.opacity = '1';
                row.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';
            });

            unitIndex++;
            bindRowEvents();
            updateSummary();
            validateForm();
        }

        function bindRowEvents() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.unit-row').forEach(row => {
                row.querySelectorAll('input, select').forEach(el => {
                    el.removeEventListener('input', onInputChange);
                    el.addEventListener('input', onInputChange);
                    el.removeEventListener('change', onInputChange);
                    el.addEventListener('change', onInputChange);
                });

                const removeBtn = row.querySelector('.remove-unit-btn');
                removeBtn.onclick = () => {
                    row.style.transition = 'all 0.2s ease';
                    row.style.opacity = '0';
                    row.style.transform = 'translateX(20px)';
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        row.remove();
                        reindexRows();
                        updateSummary();
                        validateForm();
                    }, 200);
                };

                // Hazardous checkbox handling
                const hazCheck = row.querySelector('input[type="checkbox"][name$=".IsHazardous"]');
                if (hazCheck) {
                    hazCheck.addEventListener('change', function() {
                        const hidden = this.nextElementSibling;
                        if (this.checked) {
                            hidden.disabled = true;
                        } else {
                            hidden.disabled = false;
                        }
                    });
                }
            });
        }

        function onInputChange() {
            updateSummary();
            validateForm();
        }

        function reindexRows() {
            const rows = document.querySelectorAll('.unit-row');
            rows.forEach((row, idx) => {
                row.dataset.index = idx;
                row.querySelector('.row-number').textContent = idx;
                row.querySelectorAll('input, select').forEach(input => {
                    const name = input.getAttribute('name');
                    if (name) {
                        input.setAttribute('name', name.replace(/\[\d+\]/, `[${idx}]`));
                    }
                    const id = input.getAttribute('id');
                    if (id) {
                        input.setAttribute('id', id.replace(/___INDEX__/, `__${idx}`));
                    }
                });
                row.querySelectorAll('label').forEach(label => {
                    const forAttr = label.getAttribute('for');
                    if (forAttr) {
                        label.setAttribute('for', forAttr.replace(/___INDEX__/, `__${idx}`));
                    }
                });
            });
            unitIndex = rows.length;
        }

        function updateSummary() {
            let totalUnits = 0, totalShelves = 0, totalBoxes = 0, totalCapacity = 0;
            const breakdown = [];

            document.querySelectorAll('.unit-row').forEach(row => {
                const unitsCount = parseInt(row.querySelector('.units-count').value) || 0;
                const shelves = parseInt(row.querySelector('.shelves-count').value) || 0;
                const boxes = parseInt(row.querySelector('.boxes-count').value) || 0;
                const rows = parseInt(row.querySelector('.rows-count').value) || 0;
                const cols = parseInt(row.querySelector('.cols-count').value) || 0;
                const typeSelect = row.querySelector('.unit-type-select');
                const typeText = typeSelect.selectedOptions[0]?.text || 'Unit';
                const isHazardous = row.querySelector('input[type="checkbox"][name$=".IsHazardous"]').checked;

                const shelvesInRow = unitsCount * shelves;
                const boxesInRow = shelvesInRow * boxes;
                const capacityInRow = boxesInRow * rows * cols;

                totalUnits += unitsCount;
                totalShelves += shelvesInRow;
                totalBoxes += boxesInRow;
                totalCapacity += capacityInRow;

                if (unitsCount > 0 && typeSelect.value) {
                    const hazardIcon = isHazardous ? '<i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle-fill text-danger me-1"></i>' : '';
                    breakdown.push(`
                        <div class="breakdown-item">
                            <div>
                                <span class="type-name">${hazardIcon}${typeText}</span>
                                <span class="type-count">× ${unitsCount}</span>
                            </div>
                            <span class="type-slots">${capacityInRow.toLocaleString()} slots</span>
                        </div>
                    `);
                }
            });

            document.getElementById('sumUnits').textContent = totalUnits.toLocaleString();
            document.getElementById('sumShelves').textContent = totalShelves.toLocaleString();
            document.getElementById('sumBoxes').textContent = totalBoxes.toLocaleString();
            document.getElementById('sumCapacity').textContent = totalCapacity.toLocaleString();
            document.getElementById('unitCountBadge').textContent = `${document.querySelectorAll('.unit-row').length} types`;

            const breakdownEl = document.getElementById('breakdownList');
            if (breakdown.length > 0) {
                breakdownEl.innerHTML = breakdown.join('');
            } else {
                breakdownEl.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="text-center text-muted py-3">
                        <i class="bi bi-inbox" style="font-size: 1.5rem;"></i>
                        <p class="small mt-2 mb-0">Add units to see breakdown</p>
                    </div>
                `;
            }
        }

        function validateForm() {
            const roomName = document.getElementById('roomName').value.trim();
            const hasUnits = document.querySelectorAll('.unit-row').length > 0;
            const allTypesSelected = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.unit-type-select'))
                .every(s => s.value !== '');

            generateBtn.disabled = !(roomName && hasUnits && allTypesSelected);
        }

        document.getElementById('addUnitBtn').addEventListener('click', addUnitRow);
        document.getElementById('roomName').addEventListener('input', validateForm);

        // Auto-dismiss alerts
        setTimeout(() => {
            document.querySelectorAll('.alert-floating').forEach(alert => {
                const bsAlert = bootstrap.Alert.getOrCreateInstance(alert);
                if (bsAlert) bsAlert.close();
            });
        }, 5000);

        // Start with one row
        addUnitRow();
    </script>
}

<!-- Rooms Grid -->
<div class="container-fluid room-grid" dir="ltr">
    @if (Model.RoomsList != null && Model.RoomsList.Any())
    {
        <div class="row g-4">
            @foreach (var room in Model.RoomsList)
            {
                var totalShelves = room.StorageUnits?.Sum(u => u.Shelves?.Count ?? 0) ?? 0;
                var totalBoxes = room.StorageUnits?.Sum(u => u.Shelves?.Sum(s => s.Boxes?.Count ?? 0) ?? 0) ?? 0;
                var totalSlots = room.StorageUnits?.Sum(u => u.Shelves?.Sum(s => s.Boxes?.Sum(b => b.RowsCount * b.ColumnsCount) ?? 0) ?? 0) ?? 0;

                <div class="col-xl-4 col-lg-6 col-md-6">
                    <div class="room-card rounded-4">
                        <!-- Header -->
                        <div class="room-header rounded-top-4 d-flex justify-content-between align-items-start">
                            <div class="d-flex align-items-center gap-3">
                                <div class="room-icon-wrap">
                                    <i class="bi bi-door-open-fill"></i>
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <h6 class="room-name">@room.Name</h6>
                                    <div class="room-meta">
                                        <i class="bi bi-box-seam me-1"></i>
                                        @(room.StorageUnits?.Count ?? 0) Storage Units
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <!-- FIXED: Dropdown with proper Bootstrap attributes -->
                            <div class="dropdown">
                                <button class="room-menu-btn" type="button"
                                        data-bs-toggle="dropdown"
                                        data-bs-auto-close="true"
                                        aria-expanded="false">
                                    <i class="bi bi-three-dots-vertical"></i>
                                </button>
                                <ul class="dropdown-menu room-dropdown-menu dropdown-menu-end">
                                    <li>
                                        <button type="button" class="dropdown-item" onclick="editRoom(@room.Id, '@room.Name')">
                                            <i class="bi bi-pencil text-primary"></i>Edit Room
                                        </button>
                                    </li>
                                    <li><hr class="dropdown-divider"></li>
                                    <li>
                                        <form asp-action="DeleteRoom" method="post" class="d-inline"
                                              onsubmit="return confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this room? All associated data will be removed.');">
                                            <input type="hidden" name="id" value="@room.Id" />
                                            @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                            <button type="submit" class="dropdown-item text-danger">
                                                <i class="bi bi-trash"></i>Delete Room
                                            </button>
                                        </form>
                                    </li>
                                </ul>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- Body: Storage Units -->
                        <div class="units-section">
                            @if (room.StorageUnits != null && room.StorageUnits.Any())
                            {
                                foreach (var unit in room.StorageUnits)
                                {
                                    var unitTypeName = unit.StorageUnitType?.Name ?? "Unit";
                                    var unitIcon = unit.IsHazardous ? "bi-exclamation-triangle-fill" : "bi-box-seam";
                                    var unitBadgeClass = unit.IsHazardous ? "hazardous" : "safe";
                                    var unitStatusClass = unit.IsHazardous ? "hazardous" : "safe";
                                    var unitStatusText = unit.IsHazardous ? "Hazardous" : "Safe";

                                    <div class="unit-block">
                                        <div class="unit-bar" onclick="toggleExpand(this)">
                                            <i class="bi bi-chevron-right expand-chevron"></i>
                                            <span class="unit-badge @unitBadgeClass">
                                                <i class="bi @unitIcon"></i>
                                                @unitTypeName
                                            </span>
                                            <span class="unit-name">@unit.Name</span>
                                            <span class="unit-status @unitStatusClass">
                                                <i class="bi @(unit.IsHazardous ? "bi-exclamation-triangle" : "bi-shield-check") me-1"></i>
                                                @unitStatusText
                                            </span>
                                        </div>
                                        <!-- Shelves -->
                                        <div class="shelves-wrapper">
                                            @if (unit.Shelves != null && unit.Shelves.Any())
                                            {
                                                foreach (var shelf in unit.Shelves)
                                                {
                                                    <div class="shelf-item" onclick="toggleBoxExpand(this)">
                                                        <i class="bi bi-layers"></i>
                                                        <span class="shelf-name">@shelf.Name</span>
                                                        <span class="shelf-count">
                                                            @(shelf.Boxes?.Count ?? 0) Boxes
                                                        </span>
                                                    </div>
                                                    <!-- NEW: Boxes List -->
                                                    <div class="box-list" style="display:none;">

                                                            {

                                                                    <i class="bi bi-grid-3x3"></i>
                                                                    <span class="box-name">@box.Name</span>

                                                                        @box.RowsCount × @box.ColumnsCount

                            }

@section Scripts {
    <script>
        // Toggle shelves expand/collapse
        function toggleExpand(el) {
            const shelves = el.nextElementSibling;
            const icon = el.querySelector('.expand-chevron');
            const isHidden = shelves.style.display === 'none' || !shelves.style.display || shelves.style.display === '';

            if (isHidden) {
                shelves.style.display = 'block';
                icon.classList.add('rotated');
                el.classList.add('active');
            } else {
                shelves.style.display = 'none';
                icon.classList.remove('rotated');
                el.classList.remove('active');
            }
        }

        // Toggle boxes expand/collapse within shelf
        function toggleBoxExpand(shelfEl) {
            // Find the next sibling that is a box-list
            let boxList = shelfEl.nextElementSibling;
            while (boxList && !boxList.classList.contains('box-list')) {
                boxList = boxList.nextElementSibling;
            }

            if (boxList) {
                const isHidden = boxList.style.display === 'none' || !boxList.style.display || boxList.style.display === '';
                if (isHidden) {
                    boxList.style.display = 'block';
                    shelfEl.style.borderLeftColor = 'var(--accent)';
                } else {
                    boxList.style.display = 'none';
                    shelfEl.style.borderLeftColor = '';
                }
            }
        }

        // Edit room
        function editRoom(id, name) {
            document.getElementById('roomId').value = id;
            document.getElementById('roomNameInput').value = name;
            document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Edit Room';
            var modal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('addRoomModal'));
            modal.show();
        }

        // Reset modal on close
        document.getElementById('addRoomModal').addEventListener('hidden.bs.modal', function () {
            document.getElementById('roomId').value = '0';
            document.getElementById('roomNameInput').value = '';
            document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'Add Room';
        });

        // Auto-dismiss alerts after 5 seconds
        setTimeout(() => {
            document.querySelectorAll('.alert-floating').forEach(alert => {
                var bsAlert = bootstrap.Alert.getOrCreateInstance(alert);
                if (bsAlert) bsAlert.close();
            });
        }, 5000);

        // Prevent shelf click from triggering unit expand
        document.querySelectorAll('.shelf-item').forEach(shelf => {
            shelf.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
                e.stopPropagation();
            });
        });
    </script>
}

    <script>
        let deferredPrompt;

        window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        deferredPrompt = e;

        const btn = document.getElementById('installBtn');
        if (btn) {
        btn.classList.remove('d-none');
        }
        });

        document.getElementById('installBtn')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        if (!deferredPrompt) return;

        deferredPrompt.prompt();
        const result = await deferredPrompt.userChoice;

        if (result.outcome === 'accepted') {
        console.log('User accepted install');
        }

        deferredPrompt = null;
        });
    </script>
    <script>
        window.addEventListener('appinstalled', () => {
        console.log('PWA Installed');
        const btn = document.getElementById('installBtn');
        if (btn) btn.style.display = 'none';
        });
    </script>

    <script>
        function isPwaInstalled() {
        return window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches
        || window.navigator.standalone === true;
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        if (isPwaInstalled()) {
        const btn = document.getElementById('installBtn');
        if (btn) btn.style.display = 'none';
        }
        });
    </script>
    <script>
        let deferredPrompt;
        let installModal;

        window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        deferredPrompt = e;

        installModal = new bootstrap.Modal(
        document.getElementById('installModal')
        );

        installModal.show();
        });

        document.getElementById('confirmInstall')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        if (!deferredPrompt) return;

        deferredPrompt.prompt();
        deferredPrompt = null;
        installModal.hide();
        });
    </script>
    <script>
        const installShown = localStorage.getItem('installPromptShown');

        window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', (e) => {
        if (installShown) return;

        e.preventDefault();
        deferredPrompt = e;

        localStorage.setItem('installPromptShown', 'true');

        installModal.show();
        });
    </script>

   
    @await RenderSectionAsync("Scripts", required: false)
